# RESEARCH_STATE.md

## v4.4 O1 full-8 completion COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION — post-warm-up MAE-only optimization helps broadly on the recovery panel; diagnostic reads `AUXILIARY_PERSISTENCE_BROADLY_HARMFUL_ON_RECOVERY_PANEL` (2026-09-18)

The TRAIN+VAL-only stage `experiments/current/hch_v44_o1_full8_completion_20260918/` returned `HCH_V44_O1_FULL8_COMPLETION_COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION`. The frozen O1 schedule (steps 1..400 `L_rec + (L_b+L_B+L_S)/3`, steps 401..2000 `L_rec` only, `SWITCH_STEP=400` non-tunable) was extended to the full frozen R1 recovery panel: **12 new fits** (`GANSU_DA/LSTM`, `SHANDONG_DA/PatchTST`, `SHAANXI_DA/PatchTST`, `QINGHAI_DA/TimeMixer` × seeds 7/17/37) plus the 12 existing O1 runs and all 24 R1 full-loss references reused read-only — nothing already trained was retrained. Independent verifier **16/16** without importing the runner; TEST read count **0**; `src/core` unchanged; one `probe_code_hash` (`C38EDD49…`) across all 24 runs; every O1 run reproduces its R1 reference's pre-400 checks bit-exactly.

**Gate 5/5.** Cell medians: SHANDONG_DA/PatchTST **+2.1831%**, GANSU_DA/PatchTST +1.7337%, SHAANXI_DA/PatchTST +1.2337%, QINGHAI_DA/TimeMixer +1.1678%, NINGXIA_DA/iTransformer +0.1907%, SHANDONG_DA/iTransformer +0.1903%, GANSU_DA/LSTM +0.0047%, SHAANXI_DA/TimeMixer 0.0000%. 7/8 cell medians improve (needs 6); panel median **+0.6793%** (needs ≥0.5); worst cell exactly neutral (floor −0.5); 0 step-0 retreats with selected correction/residual ratio median 0.2244 and both preregistered gate-4 readings agreeing; access, numerical, support, code and schedule checks all clean. 18/24 seeds improve. The four new cells contribute a **+1.2007%** sub-panel median against **+0.1905%** for the four reused cells, so the effect is larger on the cells that had never been probed — it is not an artefact of the reused quartet.

**Mechanism.** 20/24 runs select a post-switch checkpoint. `L_rec` falls at the final check in **24/24** runs and `L_B` rises in **24/24**; `L_b` falls in 19/24 and `L_S` rises in 16/24. The flagged `aux_worse_val_better` pattern — coordinate auxiliary error rising while VAL MAE falls — occurs in **12/24** runs at the selected horizon and 11/24 at the final horizon, confined to post-switch selections (12/20). The reverse pattern `aux_better_val_worse` occurs in **0/24** at both horizons. This is the panel-level generalization of the GANSU_DA/PatchTST finding: once coordinate supervision is removed, the balanced-mass target degrades monotonically while the actual MAE objective keeps improving.

**Scope of the verdict.** `AUXILIARY_PERSISTENCE_BROADLY_HARMFUL_ON_RECOVERY_PANEL` is a **diagnostic** statement about this TRAIN+VAL-only recovery panel: keeping the coordinate auxiliaries on for the whole budget is broadly worse for VAL MAE than switching to `L_rec` after step 400. It licenses nothing. No B-loss/Shape-loss/Level-loss removal, no router deletion, no architecture simplification and no TEST evaluation was executed or is authorized by it, and the completion token deliberately does not carry the verdict — that lives in `GATE.json`. Next action is main-window adjudication of a now-closed panel.

## v4.4 objective-alignment probe complete — persistent auxiliaries are not globally proven harmful, but a real MAE-objective conflict is exposed; bounded 8-cell O1 completion is next (2026-09-17)

The TRAIN+VAL-only stage `experiments/current/hch_v44_objective_alignment_probe_20260917/` completed 12/12 O1 fits on four fixed recovery cells and returned `HCH_V44_OBJECTIVE_ALIGNMENT_PROBE_COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION`; independent verifier 13/13, TEST reads 0, `src/core` unchanged. O1 changes exactly one thing from step-based R1: steps 1..400 use `L_rec + (L_b+L_B+L_S)/3`, while steps 401..2000 optimize `L_rec` only. The same-cell/same-seed R1 full-loss runs are reused as the reference.

**Aggregate result is positive but below the preregistered broad-harm bar.** Cell-median O1-vs-R1 VAL gains are GANSU_DA/PatchTST **+1.7337%**, NINGXIA_DA/iTransformer +0.1907%, SHANDONG_DA/iTransformer +0.1903%, SHAANXI_DA/TimeMixer +0.0000%; 3/4 cells and 9/12 seeds improve, worst cell is exactly neutral, no seed retreats to step 0, and the median selected correction/residual ratio is 0.2032. Panel median is **+0.1905%**, below the registered +0.5% condition, so the formal diagnostic verdict remains `AUXILIARY_PERSISTENCE_NOT_SHOWN_HARMFUL` (4/5), not a claim that auxiliaries are harmless.

**Mechanistic interpretation has changed.** On GANSU_DA/PatchTST, full-loss R1 is a clean objective-misalignment example: TRAIN reconstruction improves and the component targets become numerically better while VAL moves the wrong way. In seed 7, R1 from step 0→800 changes TRAIN MAE 74.711→72.635, balanced-mass error 306.7→167.5 and Shape W1 3.436→2.683, yet EMA VAL worsens 77.104→77.486 and selects step 0. Under O1, after the step-400 switch, VAL improves to 74.786 at seed-7 step 1600 while `L_B` deliberately becomes much worse (about 0.82 at step 400→3.06 at step 1600) and `L_S` does not improve. Therefore exact geometry remains a valid parameterization, but persistent coordinate-wise accuracy is **not equivalent to optimizing repaired-forecast MAE**; at least in this residual regime, forcing the balanced-mass/Shape targets can pull the estimator away from the MAE-optimal correction.

**Next bounded experiment:** do not dissect B/Shape or modify architecture yet. Complete the identical O1 schedule on the four missing cells from the already-frozen R1 8-cell panel — `GANSU_DA/LSTM`, `QINGHAI_DA/TimeMixer`, `SHAANXI_DA/PatchTST`, `SHANDONG_DA/PatchTST` — seeds 7/17/37 = **12 new fits**, reusing the 12 existing O1 fits and all 24 R1 references. This tests generality of the MAE-aligned schedule before any loss-component attribution or router simplification. V2 TEST remains quarantined. No loss-weight sweep, PCGrad/GradNorm/SAM, architecture change, router deletion, feature addition, Host/baseline rerun or paper write is authorized. Use the same diagnostic thresholds generalized to 8 cells: ≥6/8 cell medians improve, panel median O1-vs-R1 gain ≥0.5%, worst cell degradation ≤0.5%, non-degenerate correction/no step-0 retreat, and no leakage/numerical violation.

## v4.4 optimization recovery CLOSED — the step-budget repair is real but partial and non-uniform; preregistered gate not met, `NOT_SUPPORTED` (2026-09-17)

Stage `experiments/current/hch_v44_optimization_recovery_20260917/` is **CLOSED** and returned
`HCH_V44_OPTIMIZATION_RECOVERY_NOT_SUPPORTED`. Route: R1 panel did not promote, and the R2
optimization-limited trigger was not satisfied, so no tuning arm was authorized. 24/24 registered
TRAIN+VAL fits (8-cell panel × seeds 7/17/37), independent verification **12/12**, and
`test_target_read_count = 0` on every run; no TEST target, prediction or metric was read.

**R1 panel gate: 3/5.** 5/8 cells improve over the frozen recipe (gate 1 needs 6) and the panel median
improvement is **+0.2913 %** (gate 2 needs 0.5 %). Gates 3 (≥5/8 cells with selected step > 0 and
`mean|c|/mean|r| ≥ 0.05`), 4 (worst degradation −0.1478 %, floor −1.0 %) and 5 (no leakage or numerical
failure) pass. R2 trigger: no condition held in ≥50 % of runs (strongest was EMA-lags-raw at 41.7 %),
so `r2_authorized = false`.

**The recovery works exactly where the old budget was starved, and nowhere else.** Per-cell improvement
over the frozen recipe, seed-median VAL: QINGHAI_DA/TimeMixer **+4.381 %**, SHAANXI_DA/PatchTST
**+3.064 %**, NINGXIA_DA/iTransformer **+2.416 %**, GANSU_DA/LSTM +0.462 %, SHANDONG_DA/iTransformer
+0.121 %, SHAANXI_DA/TimeMixer −0.009 %, SHANDONG_DA/PatchTST **−0.148 %**, GANSU_DA/PatchTST 0.000 %.
The two largest gains are cells where the frozen recipe had essentially **zero** gain (+0.000 %), so the
step budget is the binding constraint there. But the effect is **not** a uniform repair: on
SHANDONG_DA/PatchTST — the frozen recipe's own best cell at **+1.975 %** — a longer budget is 0.148 pp
*worse*, i.e. the epoch-0 selection was not universally under-trained.

**The unrecovered cells fail for a different reason than the one that motivated the stage.** On
GANSU_DA/PatchTST all three seeds select **step 0**, and its training curve is a clean monotone
divergence: TRAIN MAE 74.711 → 72.635 with `train_correction_ratio` 0.000 → 0.126, while VAL MAE
77.104 → **77.486**, worsening at *every* check from step 100 on. The optimizer is working (loss falls,
correction grows smoothly) and the readout is not stuck; VAL simply moves the wrong way. That cell is
therefore **not** optimization-limited, and the R0 diagnosis (epoch-budget starvation) is confirmed as
**necessary but not sufficient**: where it bites, the repair is large; where it does not, more steps
make VAL worse.

**Measurement integrity.** The recovery's VAL surface reproduces the frozen stage exactly where the two
overlap: for GANSU_DA/PatchTST the new step-0 VAL MAE is 77.1041 against the frozen record's 77.1043,
with `n_val_rows` 42 in both. The verifier rebuilt the W=7 window, both eligible row sets, the day-id
hashes and the gate arithmetic independently of the runner, and proved `src/core` identity against the
digest the *completed* stage recorded before this stage existed (one prior digest, matching).

**What this is not.** No SOTA or generalization claim. V2 TEST was observed before this stage and stays
quarantined; the frozen `+0.00 %` TEST result is unchanged and immutable. The next action is a
main-window decision, not more tuning: the step-budget lever is now measured and closed.

## v4.4 post-TEST adjudication — frozen recipe is a valid negative/neutral result; optimization recovery is authorized on TRAIN+VAL only, with V2 TEST quarantined (2026-09-17)

The completed v4.4 G2 full-panel result is now adjudicated rather than merely awaiting review. Under the frozen `COMMON_BENCHMARK_701020_FULL_V2` recipe, G2 is effectively identity-like on TEST: panel median gain vs Host is `+0.00%`, it wins 16/20 but mostly by <0.1 pp, beats the best same-setting offline selected baseline only 1/20, and is worse than the simpler registered M0/M1/M2 family by roughly 1.0–6.7 pp depending on market. This negative/neutral TEST result is valid and immutable; it must not be overwritten or reinterpreted as a SOTA result.

The main-window diagnosis accepts that this is **not a plumbing failure**. A 400-step optimization probe on `GANSU_DA/PatchTST` reduces TRAIN MAE 74.7107→69.3408 and raises mean|correction| ≈0.007→18.73, proving the graph can learn. The registered recipe, however, is epoch-budgeted (`lr=1e-3`, 50 epochs, patience 8, batch 32) and therefore supplies wildly different optimizer-step budgets across markets; 34/60 runs select epoch 0 and preserve the near-zero readout. The next scientific question is therefore narrowly changed from “does v4.4 beat baselines under the frozen recipe?” to “does the same frozen v4.4 object recover under a step-based optimization budget?”

Authority is `docs/current/HCH_V44_POSTTEST_REGRESSION_ADJUDICATION_AND_RECOVERY_PLAN_20260917.md`. The new stage `experiments/current/hch_v44_optimization_recovery_20260917/` is **TRAIN+VAL only**. R1 keeps architecture, geometry, features, loss, initialization, seeds, batch, wd, dropout, clip and EMA fixed, but uses 2000 max optimizer steps, validation every 50 steps, no early stop before step 800, then patience 8 checks, with step 0 still an honest candidate checkpoint. It begins on a fixed 8-cell × 3-seed recovery panel and expands to all 20 cells only if the preregistered VAL gate passes. Conditional R2 may change exactly one optimization-path factor only if R1 is demonstrably optimization-limited; R2a is lr 3e-3, R2b is tiny Level-readout activation. No broad rescue/tuning is authorized.

Because V2 TEST has already been seen and directly motivated this recovery, **V2 TEST is quarantined from all future recovery selection**. The recovery executor may not read TEST targets, predictions, metrics, baseline TEST tables or HCH TEST overlays. Any recovered recipe requires a separately authorized untouched confirmation surface before SOTA/generalization claims. G0/G1/G3, overseas/final, optional target-day features, Host/baseline reruns, SAM/PCGrad/gate/retrieval/calibration rescue and `paper/**` writes remain closed.

## v4.4 China-5 primary full-panel Phase-A blocker adjudicated — D1=A shadow OOF registered, stage-local production harness authorized, TEST still closed (2026-09-17)

The first TEST-closed execution attempt correctly stopped at `HCH_V44_CHINA5_FULLPANEL_BLOCKED_BEFORE_FIT` with **0/60 primary fits** and **0 TEST target reads**. Two root causes were verified in `experiments/evidence/hch_v44_china5_fullpanel_main_eval_20260917/PREEXECUTION_REPORT.md`: all 20 frozen comparison Hosts were fit on the whole V2 TRAIN with VAL early stopping, so their TRAIN residuals are illegal as W=7 history and no causal OOF/prequential residual artifact exists; and the active v4.4 object has no real-data production runner outside zero-fit tests/evidence code. Main-window adjudication is now frozen in `docs/current/HCH_V44_CHINA5_FULLPANEL_UNBLOCK_ADJUDICATION_20260917.md`.

D1 chooses **Option A**. The support-only recipe `V44_SHADOW_OOF_PREFIX_90_10_V1` is registered: split each cell's V2 TRAIN target days into B0..B4, use B0 as warm-up, and for B1..B4 fit the same Host architecture/hyperparameters/registered Host seed on the strictly earlier prefix via `TorchHostAdapter.fit(prefix, valid=None)`. Its source-native chronological 90/10 prefix split is the legal shadow selection rule; the global V2 VAL segment is forbidden. Exact budget is **80 CPU shadow Host refits = 20 cells × 4 predicted blocks**. They exist only to create causal TRAIN-history residual support and never replace/report the frozen comparison Host.

D2 is also approved. A production harness may be added only under `experiments/current/hch_v44_china5_fullpanel_main_eval_20260917/implementation/**` and `/verification/**`, reusing V2 `cb_contracts/cb_cells` read-only. The old bounded-patch verifier must not mutate its historical evidence root again; repeated checks go to the current stage root. The stage identity/token sequence is unchanged: resumed Phase A must first emit `HCH_V44_CHINA5_FULLPANEL_PREEXECUTION_VERIFIED`; only then may the 60 G2 fits begin; final success token remains `HCH_V44_CHINA5_FULLPANEL_COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION`.

## Domestic Host residual archetypes supported on VAL — timing displacement and daily Level mass are both common enough to motivate v4.4 Figures 1–2 (2026-09-17)

A paper-motivation diagnostic was run on the existing `COMMON_BENCHMARK_701020` frozen Host artifacts only; no new Host/baseline/model fit and no TEST target read was performed. Across China-5 × {PatchTST, TimeMixer, iTransformer, LSTM} = 20 market×Host cells, using each artifact's VAL split, the per-cell fraction of days with a circular peak-or-valley time displacement ≥2h has median **0.900** (cell range **0.545–1.000**). The exact Level-mass ratio `eta=H|b|/||r||1=|b|/MAE_day` has an across-cell median of cell medians **0.492**; **19/20** cells have median eta≥0.3 and **9/20** have median eta≥0.5. This supports using two Introduction-level empirical phenomena—timing/placement distortion and whole-day signed Level bias—as motivation, without claiming every difficult day belongs to one archetype. Objective publishable-market examples were selected from Shaanxi VAL: PatchTST on 2025-08-24 for local timing/placement mismatch (eta≈0.257, peak shift 3h, valley shift 2h, centered shape correlation 0.689→0.836 under a diagnostic +2h circular shift) and TimeMixer on 2025-08-19 for Level bias (eta≈0.949, peak/valley shift 1h, centered shape correlation≈0.867). Analysis authority: `experiments/current/hch_paper_figure_diagnostics_20260917/analyze_host_error_archetypes.py`; paper figure blueprint: `paper/06_figures_tables/MOTIVATION_FIGURES_HOST_ERROR_ARCHETYPES_20260917.md`. These are VAL-only motivation diagnostics, not final v4.4 performance evidence.

## v4.4 clean-room bounded source patch executed and independently verified at zero fit — seven contract defects repaired, no architecture change (2026-09-17)

The bounded source patch authorised by `experiments/current/hch_v4_4_cleanroom_bounded_patch_20260917/` is **CLOSED** and returned `HCH_V4_4_CLEANROOM_BOUNDED_PATCH_READY_FOR_SCIENTIFIC_REVIEW`. Exactly the seven audited defects were repaired, with the architecture-frozen modules (`geometry.py`, `decoder.py`, `bundles.py`, `heads.py`, `encoders.py`, `feature_engineering.py`, `rescues.py`) byte-identical to the pre-patch tree and no module added, removed or newly dependent: the hour calendar is now the frozen 7 channels (hour sin/cos, weekday sin/cos, weekend, annual sin **and** cos, verified against an independent NumPy oracle on leap / non-leap / year-boundary / century dates with a sine-aliasing oracle); Shape allocation is now **same-hour source routing** with weights `[B,H,S]` softmaxed over the source axis inside each hour, so a source token at `h2` cannot move the routed Shape state or the weights at any `h1 != h2`, and the day-level softmax stays `[B,S]`; `s_r` is now the TRAIN-only robust scale of the **per-day MAE** `M1/H_valid`, so `L_rec` is a per-day MAE divided by a per-day MAE scale and agrees in unit with `L_b`/`L_B` instead of being suppressed by roughly a factor of `H` (the history residual-magnitude channel `log1p((M1/H_valid)/s_r)` was moved to the same unit, and no loss weight or scale hyperparameter was added); `build_history_tensors` now stacks and threads each history day's own valid mask into the Shape aggregation, so `±1e6` invalid filler is bitwise invisible; the structured and G3 reconstruction terms are valid-mask aware, rows with `valid_count == 0` are excluded from the row-level `b/B` means, and `model.require_full_daily_support` is a fail-closed guard requiring 24/24 eligible hours for the first scientific run (deliberately not applied inside `forward`, so synthetic partial-support tests still run); `training_support.fp32_region()` now disables autocast for the **active** device type (CPU and CUDA) instead of CPU only; and the never-used trainable `MaskedSoftmaxAllocator.routed` projection was deleted rather than repurposed. Parameter counts are now G0 = G1 = G2 = **14948** and G3 = **16897** (G3/G2 = 1.1304, budget 1.25), the change coming only from the removed dead Linear and the calendar projection's extra input channel. 125 maintained `test_v44_cleanroom_*` tests pass and 12 independent verification checks pass, the verifier re-implementing the calendar oracle, the same-hour routing softmax, the per-day-MAE scale, the mass-weighted Shape-history aggregation and the repeated-horizon unit oracle in NumPy/float64 rather than importing the builder. `scientific_neural_fit_count=0`, `test_target_value_read_count=0`, `paper_mutation_count=0`, `host_or_baseline_retrain_count=0`; the previous stage's evidence root is unmodified. Two items are recorded and explicitly **not** changed: the Coordinate Headroom Audit's random K-fold assignment and the still-unconsumed holiday/day-type history channels remain future extension follow-ups, and `docs/current/HCH_V4_4_CLEANROOM_CORE_MAIN_WINDOW_AUDIT_20260917.md` — cited by this file, `HANDOFF.md`, `experiments/STAGE_INDEX.md` and `docs/current/README.md` — **does not exist on disk**, so the seven-item defect list in those three state files was used instead and the missing document is reported as a documentation defect. This token authorises main-window source review only: no TRAIN/VAL scientific fit, no TEST/QINGHAI access, no `foreign`/`final` run, no Host/baseline rerun, no paper edit and no 96-fit G0-G3 ladder. Evidence root: `experiments/evidence/hch_v4_4_cleanroom_bounded_patch_20260917/` (start with `RESULTS.md`).

## v4.4 clean-room core main-window audit found bounded source defects; selected China-5 baseline completion opened in parallel (2026-09-17)

The returned clean-room source token is not yet scientific-execution authority. Main-window source review reran the maintained clean-room suite (`105 passed`) but found contract-level defects documented in `docs/current/HCH_V4_4_CLEANROOM_CORE_MAIN_WINDOW_AUDIT_20260917.md`: hour calendar omits annual cosine despite the frozen sin/cos contract; Shape allocation currently attends across all `S*H` source-hours rather than selecting among same-hour evidence sources; reconstruction divides per-hour MAE by a daily-L1 `s_r` scale; historical Shape aggregation drops per-day valid masks; reconstruction loss ignores valid support; `fp32_region()` disables CPU rather than active-device autocast; and the allocator contains one unused trainable projection. The scientific architecture itself remains supported as the development object. One source-only bounded patch is authorized by `experiments/current/hch_v4_4_cleanroom_bounded_patch_20260917/`; zero scientific fits/TEST reads remain required until its token is re-audited.

## v4.4 design stage closed — final scientific object and first ladder frozen; clean-room source reproduction is next (2026-09-16)

## Round-8 v4.4 convergence — learned Market Core/sparsity are removed from the primary path; joint geometry training gets an optimizer-diagnostic guardrail (2026-09-16)

Further literature/design review now closes three remaining architecture questions without reopening experiments. Deep Sets / Set Transformer show that learned attention pooling is a valid generic primitive, but the primary v4.4 bank contains only three typed legal bundles (Host, revealed residual geometry history, calendar). A learned PMA/Perceiver-style Market Core would duplicate selection before the actual geometry-conditioned allocator and weaken the matched G1/G2 test. The primary design therefore keeps local bundle tokens intact and uses only deterministic masked-mean+normalization as query-conditioning context. Likewise, entmax/Top-K/Hard-Concrete sparsity is deferred: exact sparsity is useful for large noisy pools, but with three sources ordinary masked softmax is lower-risk and isolates the scientific question. Sparse routing remains an optional future rich-exogenous extension, not part of the first candidate.

The joint-vs-sequential decision also remains **joint**. Exact `b/B/S+/S-` targets are deterministic coordinates of one Host residual, and coordinate-perfect prediction has the same optimum as exact reconstruction, so the semantic conflict that motivated earlier frozen/sequential Level routes is absent. Generic MTL work (GradNorm, PCGrad, CAGrad, Nash-MTL, MTAN/Cross-Stitch) is retained only as an optimization warning: the clean-room implementation should log gradient norms/cosines among reconstruction, Level, B and Shape objectives on shared parameters. Persistent strong negative interference may justify a preregistered implementation-only PCGrad/GradNorm rescue, but no gradient method is a contribution and no sequential Level is reopened by default.

A second Shape audit finds that an **uncentered** single signed field is algebraically target-surjective and would enforce disjoint predicted sign supports, but ReLU sign partitioning creates dead-support optimization regions, while smooth relaxations restore overlap and hard sign assignment adds complexity. The safer first candidate remains one shared Shape trunk + two independent 24h simplex heads, with cancellation overlap logged only. Target-mass-weighted W1 is retained strictly as a within-day placement auxiliary; reconstruction MAE remains primary. The literature map now extends through §§53–59. Current `src/core` remains non-authoritative; no source rebuild or scientific fit is authorized by this round.

## Round-7 v4.4 deepening — correction-feature literature tightens novelty; centered one-field Shape is rejected; current core is non-authoritative (2026-09-16)

The v4.4 literature/design round has materially tightened both method and implementation authority. New direct correction precedents include COSA (ICLR 2026), ORCA, CRAFTER, Foundation-MoE, RATL, TS-Memory, NSR-Boost and ICML-2026 Black-Box Assisted Regression. These close off broad claims around context-aware frozen-output correction, residual-targeted feature discovery, sparse/horizon-token residual routing, residual memory/retrieval and hard-region residual experts. Foundation-MoE is especially close in Chinese provincial EPF, so HCH should avoid the phrase **sparse residual routing**. The surviving paper-facing hypothesis is now deliberately narrow: `exact Host-residual geometry -> geometry-induced repair coordinates -> coordinate-specific legal evidence allocation -> exact target reconstruction`. Prefer **geometry-aligned evidence allocation** / **geometry-conditioned context allocation** as terminology.

CRAFTER creates an important implementation shift: feature engineering for a frozen postprocessor should target the **Host failure process**, not raw-price predictability. A future legal-covariate extension should therefore use a TRAIN-only `Coordinate Headroom Audit` against exact `b/B/Shape` targets before the neural allocator: Level and balanced-amplitude headroom separately, plus horizon/mass-weighted Shape headroom. Domestic EPF work supports a small catalogue of market-semantic derived candidates such as renewable penetration, load-renewable margin, net bidding-space ratios, ramps, event flags and uncertainty/spread features. This remains implementation-only effectiveness engineering, not a method contribution or result-conditioned gate.

A new first-principles Shape audit rejects the previously frozen **centered one-field signed-simplex** output. Hard centering cannot represent all one-signed residual targets; e.g. an all-positive residual whose positive Shape has support on every hour cannot be the positive normalized part of a nonzero centered score field. The initial v4.4 Shape parameterization is now two independently normalized 24h Shape heads with a shared trunk. Exact Jordan target Shapes remain mutually singular, so perfect coordinates still reconstruct exactly; predicted overlap is allowed and logged through `C_overlap=(A+ + A-) - ||A+S+ - A-S-||1 >=0`, not penalized in the first run. The target identity `||r||1=H|b|+2B` and prediction daily-sum identity `sum(c_hat)=H*b_hat` remain exact, while predicted total variation is an inequality unless predicted supports are disjoint. The Shape auxiliary is now frozen as **target-mass-weighted W1**, `(P W1+ + N W1-)/(P+N+eps)`, not equal active-sign averaging.

## v4.4 feature audit adjudication — conservative ADMIT=0 stands, audit package itself is incomplete; primary architecture pivots to always-legal Host+history context (2026-09-16)

The first zero-fit feature-availability run returned `HCH_V4_4_FEATURE_AVAILABILITY_INSUFFICIENT_STOP_FOR_ADJUDICATION` with 40 manifest rows and zero scientific fits / TEST target reads / outcome-driven feature selections. Its scientific safety decision is accepted: **no target-day provincial `预测值` column is admitted to v4.4 at present**, so the method must not depend on those trajectories. However the run is not accepted as a protocol-complete availability audit. Repository review found only one date-mask file (`QINGHAI_DA`) although five were required; `VERIFICATION_REPORT.json` itself reports `mask_files=1`; `EVIDENCE_INDEX.json` is only a placeholder unresolved item; G1/G2/G3 HOLD statuses were hard-coded by the audit runner instead of independently evaluated; derived-feature rows were not numerically tested; and the verifier mostly checked the blanket HOLD state. Several date-span numbers in the user-facing summary also differ from the actual source/manifest endpoints. Thus the zero-admission conclusion is conservative and usable, but the evidence package requires a bounded zero-fit repair before it can be called complete.

This result simplifies rather than blocks the scientific architecture. The primary v4.4 path no longer requires target-day exogenous trajectories, Market-Core aggregation or entmax sparsification. The exact Level–Balanced-Amplitude–signed-Shape geometry remains unchanged. Primary legal information is now: (i) the complete frozen-Host 24h prediction in absolute and within-day-relative views, (ii) deterministic calendar/hour information with date-library semantics rather than epoch-modulo shortcuts, and (iii) W=7 strictly revealed residual history with reveal-time < current forecast origin and causal OOF/prequential TRAIN warm start. Current Host is encoded once by a small BiGRU32. Historical residuals are decomposed with the same exact v4.4 geometry; day-level `(b,B)` history uses one small causal GRU32, while Shape history uses deterministic active-sign per-hour prototypes/dispersion rather than KNN/global retrieval. Geometry-specific Level/B/Shape queries allocate this same small evidence bank differently using a minimal softmax/context fusion; entmax becomes optional future-exogenous engineering, not a primary contribution. A new first-principles clarification is also frozen: the exact decoder is surjective onto all residual vectors, so it has no zero-sum-style representation floor. Under MAE the unconstrained deterministic target is the horizon-wise conditional median of residual given the legal information set; therefore any gap to a matched direct head is a learnability/conditioning question, not expressivity. A simple L1 bound shows reconstruction error is controlled by `2|B_hat-B| + 2H|b_hat-b|` plus positive/negative mass-weighted Shape errors, giving the coordinate auxiliaries a direct optimization interpretation while reconstruction MAE remains the task-defining loss.

A final first-principles pass strengthens the exact geometry before source implementation. Because `P+N=||r||_1` and `|P-N|=H|b|`, the balanced amplitude satisfies `B=(||r||_1-H|b|)/2`, hence the headline-MAE mass identity `||r||_1=H|b|+2B`. Also, any decoded correction has `sum(c_hat)=H*b_hat`, so daily repair MAE is lower-bounded by `|b*-b_hat|`; Shape cannot compensate for a wrong net Level. This supplies a direct MAE interpretation for the Level/B coordinates rather than treating them as heuristic heads. The predicted Shape parameterization is tightened from two independent sign-softmaxes to one centered signed hour field `u`: `S+=u_+/sum u_+`, `S-=(-u)_+/sum(-u)_+`. Positive/negative supports are therefore disjoint, preventing same-hour cancellation from destroying the intended Jordan mass semantics and restoring `||c_hat||_1=2B_hat+H|b_hat|` for active masses. This refinement is merged into `docs/current/HCH_V4_4_PREFREEZE_CLOSURE_20260916.md`.

The post-audit development ladder remains narrowed to four variants on the existing 8-cell TRAIN+VAL panel, seeds 7/17/37: `G0_GEOM_HOST` (Host+calendar only), `G1_GEOM_SHARED_CONTEXT` (same legal residual-history context but one shared mixture), primary `G2_GEOM_COORD_CONTEXT` (coordinate-specific allocation), and `G3_DIRECT_CONTEXT_CTRL` (same legal information/capacity but direct 24h residual output). This would be 96 new postprocessor fits if later explicitly authorized. Raw output remains primary; no zero-sum rescue, retrieval, safety gate, entmax ablation, deeper-capacity rescue, market expert or dual-static headline is allowed after seeing outcomes. A bounded repair prompt for the feature audit is at `experiments/current/hch_v4_4_feature_availability_audit_20260916/BOUNDED_REPAIR_PROMPT.md`; it must not train the model or promote features without genuine G1+G2+G3 evidence.

## v4.4 residual-question closure — closest-work audit complete; only zero-fit feature-admission manifest remains before experiment freeze (2026-09-16)

The four Round-6 residual design questions are now separated cleanly. (1) The one-signed Shape convention is frozen: each sign's Shape loss is masked exactly when its target sign mass is zero; both-zero residual days contribute zero Shape loss. (2) The minimal 24h future-known encoder is frozen as one shared one-layer family-conditioned BiGRU (total output width 32), producing both a day token and hour-aligned states; bidirectionality is legal only because the full target-day forecast trajectory must already be available at forecast origin. (3) The dedicated closest-work collision audit is complete at `docs/current/HCH_V4_4_CLOSEST_WORK_COLLISION_AUDIT_20260916.md`: δ-Adapter, DARC, MTAN, DUET, Adapformer, SOFTS/STAR, CATS, TimeXer, NBEATSx, entmax and sparse-gating work occupy the individual primitives, so the only defensible development hypothesis is the coupling `exact Host-residual geometry -> geometry-derived repair queries -> coordinate-specific routing of legal forecast-origin evidence -> exact reconstruction`. No further architecture-family search is required before the development experiment. (4) Issue-time legality is the only unresolved pre-fit item, and it is now a data/provenance task rather than a model-design task.

Fresh primary-source review strengthens the issue-time prior but does not replace the manifest. The 2024 national `电力市场信息披露基本规则` explicitly treats system-load, supply-demand, intertie, total-generation, non-market-generation, renewable and hydro/pumped-storage forecasts as market prediction information. Gansu's current official disclosure rules publish delivery-day system load, intertie, renewable and hydro boundary forecasts before day-ahead bidding (current text: D-2 20:00; another current trading-rule version gives D-2 22:00 for boundary release). Shaanxi's 2026 continuous-operation plan requires renewable sites to submit next-day 96-point forecasts by 08:00, publishes ex-ante boundary information by 09:00 and closes day-ahead declarations at 09:30. Ningxia/Qinghai official/market materials likewise establish load/new-energy forecasts as day-ahead/realtime boundary objects, but concrete workbook-column provenance and historical effective-date coverage are still not proven. Therefore `预测值` columns remain HOLD until the zero-fit three-gate audit (`RULE_CATEGORY_LEGAL + DATASET_COLUMN_MAPPED + DATE_RANGE_COVERED`) returns a frozen date-aware mask.

A zero-fit audit stage is prepared at `experiments/current/hch_v4_4_feature_availability_audit_20260916/`; terminal token is `HCH_V4_4_FEATURE_AVAILABILITY_AUDIT_COMPLETE` or the insufficient-evidence stop token. No v4.4 scientific fit, TEST/foreign/final opening or paper update is authorized yet.

## Round-6 convergence — exact Level–Balanced-Amplitude–Shape geometry resolves the joint-vs-sequential dilemma; v4.4 now has a near-final mathematical backbone (2026-09-16)

A new exact residual parameterization has emerged from the post-USM evidence and closest-work review. For any frozen-Host residual `r`, define signed Level `b=mean(r)`, positive/negative masses `P=sum(r+)`, `N=sum((-r)+)`, balanced amplitude `B=min(P,N)`, and normalized signed Shapes `S+`,`S-`. Because `P-N=H*b`, exactly `P=B+[H*b]+`, `N=B+[-H*b]+`, hence `r=(B+[H*b]+)S+-(B+[-H*b]+)S-`. This is an exact reparameterization of the unbalanced Jordan signed-mass geometry. It gives one unique Level/net-mass coordinate, one balanced redistribution amplitude, and signed within-day Shapes. `b=0` exactly recovers the old zero-sum model.

This resolves the previous architecture conflict: v3.9 spread Level uniformly; v4.0 allowed duplicate mean-capable Level/Local degrees; v4.1 froze Level and made Local predict an error-of-error remainder; USM was expressive but lost the original Level/Amplitude/Shape semantics. In the new geometry, coordinate-perfect predictions and reconstruction-perfect predictions share the same optimum, so the Host can remain frozen while the whole postprocessor is again trained jointly end-to-end on the original Host residual. N-BEATS/DeepBooTS are precedents for end-to-end residual/decomposition training; they are not claimed as novelty. A dedicated design record is `docs/current/HCH_V4_4_GEOMETRY_ROUTING_DEEP_DESIGN_20260916.md`.

The multivariate router has also narrowed. The Market Core should be a deterministic masked-mean/DeepSets-style context prior over projected semantic tokens, with all feature-local tokens preserved; do not duplicate selection inside the core. The leading sparse routing operator is fixed 1.5-entmax because it gives exact zeros while remaining differentiable and avoids a hard K, Gumbel schedule or keep-rate budget. This helps distinguish the implementation from δ-Adapter (ICLR 2026), which already uses a sparse horizon-aware Gumbel-Sigmoid mask with L1/entropy/TV/budget penalties. MTAN also already covers global shared features + generic task-specific attention. The defensible HCH object is therefore geometry-induced multi-resolution repair queries: one Level/net-mass query, one balanced-amplitude query, and horizon-specific Shape queries over the same legal semantic feature pool/core.

Repository header audit materially strengthens feasibility: Gansu and Ningxia each contain 8 `预测值` trajectories, Shaanxi 9 and Qinghai 5; five are common to all four public provincial files (total renewable, hydro+pumped-storage output, directly controlled load, non-market-unit total output, bidding-space forecasts). Wind/PV/tie-line/total-generation appear in the union; private Shandong has ten forecast-value columns. Recent CSEE 2026 Shanxi work explicitly supports net spot-market bidding capacity derived from load/bidding information as a useful price feature. However, `预测值` naming alone does not prove forecast-origin legality; a market-specific issue-time availability manifest is still mandatory before v4.4 execution. No experiment is authorized yet.

## Round-5 literature deep read — v4.4 is converging toward repair-coordinate sparse cross-routing with preserved feature tokens (2026-09-16)

The literature map `docs/current/HCH_MTSF_CAPACITY_FEATURE_NETMASS_LITERATURE_MAP_20260916.md` now extends through §37. The multivariate design has narrowed materially. Explicit learned feature clustering is no longer the leading choice because DUET already performs Channel-Soft-Clustering+sparsification, TimeCAP performs channel grouping+meta-routing, and generic MMoE/PLE literature already covers task-specific gating over shared representations. The more defensible HCH-specific mechanism is now **repair-coordinate query routing**: preserve semantic feature-local tokens, build one compact market-state core as context rather than as a destructive bottleneck, and let mathematically defined repair coordinates query the feature pool differently.

CATS (NeurIPS 2024) provides the strongest new architectural precedent for Shape: each future horizon can be treated as an independent query using cross-attention rather than another full self-attention stack. TAT (ICDE 2026) independently supports explicit alignment of difficult future horizons with known-future context. The provisional query layout is therefore one day-level Level query, one day-level Mass query, and 24 horizon-specific Shape queries. SOFTS/STAR supports centralized market-core context while preserving local channel representations; Adapformer and Li-Net support actual sparse covariate/channel use rather than dense interaction with all noisy variables.

The current near-final skeleton remains discussion-only: `semantic legal feature tokens + compact market core -> repair-coordinate sparse cross-routing -> Level/Mass/24-Shape queries -> structured repair decoder`. Future-known exogenous trajectories must stay semantically separate and horizon-aligned; an issue-time feature-availability audit is required before any claim assumes load/wind/solar/schedule forecasts are legal. Zero-sum is not reopened: retain unbalanced signed-mass geometry and use conservative treatment for weak net mass. Similar-day prototypes, jump flags, SHAP, optimizer/SAM and other last-mile tricks remain implementation-only. No v4.4 architecture or scientific experiment is yet frozen/authorized.

## Round-4 literature deep read — explicit clustering downgraded; coordinate-selective channel management becomes the leading v4.4 research abstraction (2026-09-16)

The literature map has been extended through Round 4. Generic learned channel grouping is now judged too crowded to serve as the v4.4 scientific core: DUET learns soft/sparse channel affinity, TimeCAP uses channel grouping + meta-routing, Adapformer selects Top-K relevant covariates per forecast target, and additional recent work learns latent/hierarchical channel structures. Generic task-specific feature selection is also an established multi-task-learning idea. Therefore explicit K-group clustering is downgraded from the preferred candidate.

The current leading abstraction is simpler and more HCH-specific: **semantic legal feature tokens -> compact shared market-state core -> repair-coordinate-specific sparse routing -> Level / Shape / Mass heads**. Feature semantic type is retained as an embedding/prior, but functional grouping is learned implicitly through routing signatures rather than hard groups. Level/Mass use day-level feature relevance; Shape may use horizon-conditioned feature relevance, consistent with domestic EPF evidence that feature importance changes strongly by hour. The selector should be an actual multiplicative information bottleneck (gate/sparse normalized mixture/Top-K), not ordinary self-attention weights treated as explanation. Any interpretability claim must be cross-checked by ablation/occlusion/SHAP-style analysis.

Adapformer and TFT strengthen the case for target/instance-specific covariate selection; SOFTS motivates a compact shared core. DAG (ICML 2026) adds an important boundary for true future-known exogenous forecasts: historical endogenous, historical exogenous and future exogenous information should remain semantically distinct, and future-exogenous trajectories should be encoded/aligned before routing rather than simply concatenated. Full DAG-style correlation discovery is considered too large for the first v4.4 build. LassoNet/L-NBeatsX show that nonlinear neural feature sparsity is already established and can be implementation support, not novelty.

Novelty boundary is tightened: HCH cannot claim feature selection, sparse attention, channel grouping, global-core aggregation, Top-K channel selection or common/task-specific feature sharing by themselves. The most defensible paper-facing object, if later supported, is **repair-coordinate-conditioned information routing over forecast-origin context**, coupled with the already-established non-zero-sum signed-mass Local geometry. Implementation-only tricks remain outside contributions. No new fit or v4.4 freeze is authorized yet.

## Round-3 literature deep read — learned feature grouping/routing is preferred over hard selection as the paper-facing multivariate mechanism (2026-09-16)

The literature map `docs/current/HCH_MTSF_CAPACITY_FEATURE_NETMASS_LITERATURE_MAP_20260916.md` has been extended with a third deep-reading round focused on automatic feature grouping and adaptive noise suppression. The strongest precedents now separate into three families: TFT-style instance-wise variable selection, DUET-style learned soft channel clustering+sparsification, and SOFTS/TimeCAP-style global-core/meta-router aggregation and redistribution. Domestic EPF evidence (Shandong Applied Energy 2024, HE-DTimeXer 2026, Guangdong Applied Energy 2026, EP-Net 2026, ATTnet and related work) still supports a minimal TRAIN-only legality/coarse-denoise layer, but the final relevance assignment should preferentially be learned end-to-end when data allow.

The current provisional v4.4 abstraction is therefore no longer `static filter -> selector -> heads` as a hard scientific pipeline. The preferred paper-facing form is `legal feature tokens -> learned soft grouping/sparse relevance -> global market-state core -> task-conditioned redistribution -> Level/Shape/Mass`. A TRAIN-only static screen remains implementation hygiene only: remove invalid, constant, duplicated or obviously redundant variables and enforce issue-time availability, but do not let XGBoost/MIC define the scientific representation unless necessary for stability. Two candidate core mechanisms remain under review: (A) a simpler SOFTS/TFT-inspired global core with task-specific gates; (B) a more novel DUET/TimeCAP-inspired latent soft-group router with K learned group prototypes and three repair-coordinate queries. Candidate B better matches the desire for automatic feature categorization but has greater novelty-collision/complexity risk; Candidate A is safer and easier to ablate. No final v4.4 architecture is frozen yet.

A new innovation boundary is also fixed: the claim cannot be "attention selects useful features" or "variables are clustered" because these primitives already exist. The defensible HCH-specific object, if supported later, is **repair-coordinate-conditioned information routing**: heterogeneous forecast-origin variables form a shared market-state representation but are redistributed differently to residual Level, signed Shape and signed Mass correction coordinates. Implementation-only tricks (similar-day statistics, tree/MIC coarse filters, holiday encoding, optimizer/SAM, calibration, jump flags, caching) remain outside the claimed method and are used only for effectiveness. No new scientific fit is authorized by this literature update.

## Literature synthesis shifts next-method question from generic capacity to structured interaction + predictable net mass (2026-09-16)

A first large-scale literature pass has been completed and frozen in `docs/current/HCH_MTSF_CAPACITY_FEATURE_NETMASS_LITERATURE_MAP_20260916.md`. Across TimeRecipe (ICLR 2026), the TKDE capacity/robustness analysis, TSMixer/MTS-Mixers/Crossformer/iTransformer, SOFTS, TimeXer, Li-Net (ICDE 2026), TAT (ICDE 2026), SAMformer and related work, the consistent lesson for HCH is that network complexity should be placed at a named dependency — temporal mixing, cross-variable fusion, sparse channel selection or exogenous-to-target alignment — rather than increased as undifferentiated depth/width. The latest U1/U3 evidence is therefore interpreted as rejecting generic capacity as the leading bottleneck, not as rejecting all multivariate interaction modeling.

Feature engineering is now treated as an implementation/data-contract problem unless a transformation is itself the scientific mechanism. A second domestic-market literature pass strengthens this into a three-layer pattern: **TRAIN-only static/coarse relevance filtering -> online adaptive variable weighting -> horizon/task-specific interaction**. Shandong 2024 uses similar-day construction plus XGBoost embedded selection; HE-DTimeXer combines PCC/MIC-TSA screening with holiday encoding and differential attention; Guangdong 2026 combines MIC screening with SHAP validation; robust EPF work uses 24 hourly XGBoost selectors to avoid washing out hour-specific importance; EP-Net uses XGBoost preselection followed by learned channel gating. The emerging implementation target is therefore an availability contract plus one offline selector/prior and one online selector/core, not a large unfiltered feature tensor. The next legal feature contract, if later authorized, should preserve absolute and relative Host views, strictly forecast-origin exogenous point forecasts, forecast uncertainty/quantiles where available, deterministic calendar channels with date-oracle tests, strictly revealed residual histories and explicit availability masks. The current USM run did not test the original load/renewable/market-variable interaction hypothesis because those legal forecast-known covariates were absent from its authorized boundary.

The multivariate architecture direction is also narrowed but not yet frozen as v4.4: encode legal variable groups locally, aggregate selected information into one compact **global market-state core** (SOFTS/STAR-style centralized interaction), then redistribute task-specific projections to Level/Shape/mass heads. Future-known exogenous variables should remain semantically separate from historical observations, following TimeXer/TAT. Sparse Top-K, TFT-style variable selection, SOFTS redistribution and differential attention are alternative selector mechanisms; the final design should choose one, not stack them.

The zero-sum interpretation is further sharpened: `q=A+S+−A−S−` resolves the representation restriction, but `D=A+−A−=sum(q)=H*mean(q)` remains weakly observable. Black-Box Assisted Regression provides a direct analogue in which a residual correction can exist yet not be statistically worth learning, and Corrected Forecast Combinations separates forecast-origin-predictable error from innovation. Because v4.4 is intended as a near-final design, the zero-sum component should not trigger another long research branch: keep the exact unbalanced signed-mass geometry, but consider a conservative shrink-to-zero prior/gate on `D` rather than a new coordinate system. Candidate last-mile engineering still under literature review includes similar-day context, per-horizon feature selection, holiday/special-event encoding, jump/volatility side signals, compact optimization fixes and probabilistic exogenous uncertainty; none is execution-authorized yet.

## USM capacity ladder executed; post-execution code audit finds calendar-contract defect, so final token remains withheld (2026-09-16)

`HCH_UNBALANCED_SIGNED_MASS_CAPACITY_20260916` completed all 96 registered Stage-2 fits with TEST/joint/QINGHAI/foreign/final closed. The independent verifier reproduced the raw/gate numbers but correctly withheld `HCH_USM_CAPACITY_LADDER_COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION` because D3's §11 coordinate block decomposed the composed full correction rather than D3's emitted Local vector and reported tied RCL Shapes rather than Jordan Shapes. That D29 defect affects 24/96 D3 diagnostic blocks and no registered gate.

Source audit now finds a second, scientifically more important registered-input defect in `implementation/usm_features.py`: `dow = days_since_epoch % 7` is used directly even though epoch day zero is Thursday, and `is_weekend = dow >= 5`. This maps Tuesday/Wednesday to weekend and Saturday/Sunday to non-weekend. U0/U1 are unaffected; U2/U3/D3 were trained on the wrong binary calendar feature. In addition, PROTOCOL §6 defined U2 as dual Host + raw-residual summary, but the executed U2 token also added seven calendar channels. Therefore the current `CONTEXT_EXPANSION_SUPPORTED` label cannot be promoted as evidence for the intended U2 context design without a bounded replay; the internal U3-vs-U2 and U3-vs-D3 contrasts remain informative because both arms share the same calendar substrate, but they are not executions of the registered feature semantics.

The network-capacity conclusion itself is negative/weak rather than a failure of implementation: U1 is a real ~6.1× expansion of frozen R2 (135,524 vs 22,244 parameters) and preserves U0's TRAIN/VAL input, target and loss arithmetic bit-for-bit, yet gives only 5/8 wins, panel median +0.0503 pp, and D-MAE improvement in 3/8. U3 grows to 387,428 parameters but adds only +0.0239 pp over U2. U1-vs-U0 is not a literally one-factor comparison because the successor recipe uses 60 epochs / patience 10 versus v4.1's 50/8; this should be disclosed if making a publication-grade capacity claim. The stronger lesson is that generic depth/width is not the current bottleneck.

On zero-sum, the implementation does genuinely remove the hard representation constraint for U1/U2/U3: `local=A+S+−A−S−` with independent nonnegative masses and masked-simplex Shapes, and the emission identity verifies. But the net-mass coordinate is still essentially unlearned. With H=24, `D=A+−A−=sum(q)=24*mean(q)`, so this is exactly the former daily-mean remainder on the sum scale. The best variant in every cell is within 3.1% of the `D_hat=0` reference and six cells within 0.9%; U1 is worse than zero in 6/8. Thus breaking zero-sum solves representation capacity but does not solve forecast-origin predictability of signed net mass. This strengthens the prior v4.1 conclusion that the bottleneck is observability/conditional predictability, not the particular `delta` parameterization.

Also do not promote the current RESULTS.md claim that smaller B-MAE than E-MAE proves target residuals are excess-dominated: MAE magnitudes are prediction errors, not target mass composition. Target `B*`/`E*` summaries would be required for that claim. Controlling audit: `docs/current/HCH_USM_CAPACITY_POSTEXECUTION_CODE_AUDIT_20260916.md`. No new architecture or TEST opening is authorized. A bounded repair, if explicitly approved, should first correct calendar semantics + D3 coordinate reporting and replay only calendar-affected U2/U3/D3 (72 fits); U0/U1 need not be replayed for those defects.

## Unbalanced signed-mass Local + capacity/interaction ladder is the next development design (2026-09-16)

The v4.0↔v4.1 reconciliation is now complete and removes the main protocol confound: v4.0 `C2_RAW`'s historical +0.984% headline was already dual-static calibrated, while v4.1 R1 headline was raw. On matched raw output v4.0 is better by `0.8206 MAE`; on matched dual calibration the sign reverses and v4.1 is better by `0.4145`. `R_cal=0.4948857` is just below the preregistered DOMINANT threshold, so calibration is correctly labelled `MATERIAL`, not DOMINANT. Level-object and Local-emission gaps are SMALL, history information loss is NOT_DETECTED, absolute Host-level confounding is PLAUSIBLE_ONLY, and parameterization remains UNRESOLVED. The discrepancy is concentrated mainly in the daily-mean coordinate, not the centered Shape coordinate.

This closes the argument for repeatedly redesigning the mean scalar. The next first-principles candidate removes the explicit v4.1 `delta` head and lets Local represent the post-Level remainder exactly as an **unbalanced signed mass**: `q=A+S+−A−S−`. Equal masses recover the old zero-sum model as a special case; unequal masses supply the missing nonzero-mean freedom. For interpretation, `B=min(A+,A−)` is balanced redistribution mass and `|A+−A−|` is unbalanced excess, analogous to transport versus mass creation/destruction in unbalanced optimal transport. No Sinkhorn/OT solver is added.

A controlled capacity ladder is frozen in `docs/current/HCH_UNBALANCED_SIGNED_MASS_INTERACTION_DESIGN_20260916.md` and `experiments/current/hch_unbalanced_signed_mass_capacity_20260916/`. It reuses the verified v4.1 Stage-1 Level and exact LOCAL_TRAIN/CAL/EVAL support. U1 changes **only Stage-2 capacity** relative to frozen v4.1 R2; U2 adds absolute+relative Host views and compact raw-residual regime summaries; U3 adds a deeper dense feature mixer plus legal 24h bidirectional GRU interaction encoder; D3 is a matched-capacity direct-q control. Raw output is primary; shared/dual calibration is secondary diagnostic only. New fit budget is fixed at 96. No TEST/QINGHAI/foreign/final/paper continuation is automatic.

## RCL v4.1 scientific campaign completed — residual-complete delta closure NOT SUPPORTED as the leading mechanism (2026-09-16)

`HCH_RESIDUAL_COMPLETE_LOCAL_V4_1_20260915` has completed under the authorized 8-cell TRAIN+VAL campaign. Phase-0 returned `RCL_V4_1_FINAL_PREEXECUTION_GATE_PASS` with 182/182 tests, 0 scientific fits and 0 TEST reads before execution. The campaign then completed exactly 216 neural fits: 96 causal Stage-1 OOF Level fits + 24 final-Level refits + 96 Stage-2 fits. Independent verifier PASS: 4733 checks, 0 failed; TEST target read count remained 0, `host_predictions_joint.npz` was never opened, QINGHAI/foreign/final were not read, Hosts/baselines were not rerun, and `src/core` digest remained unchanged. Terminal token: `HCH_RCL_V4_1_SCIENTIFIC_EXECUTION_COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION`. Evidence root: `experiments/evidence/hch_residual_complete_local_v4_1_20260915/`.

Primary R1 (`RCL_ORTHOGONAL`) does **not** support the hypothesis that an explicit Local `delta` head can reliably close the coarse-Level daily-mean remainder under the current forecast-origin information. R1 vs matched hard-zero-sum R0 is only 4/8 non-worse with panel-median gain `-0.0249%`, worst `-1.5418%`; all five preregistered R1-vs-R0 support gates fail. `delta` beats the zero-delta reference in only 2/8 cells and panel mean-floor reduction is `-1.934%`. The panel closure ratio is `1.049661` (>1), so the learned delta worsens rather than recovers the median Stage-1 mean miss. R1 vs Host also misses its development target: 3/8 positive, panel median `-0.2685%`, worst `-1.9951%`. The large GANSU/LSTM Host gain (`+11.503%`) is not evidence for delta closure because R0 is already strong there; R1 adds only `+1.367%` over R0.

The coordinate comparison is likewise unresolved rather than supportive. R1 vs native untied signed-mass R2 is exactly mixed (4/8 each; panel median R1 improvement `-0.0144%`), so the orthogonal `delta + A(S+−S−)` coordinate is not established as superior to direct `A+S+−A−S−`. R3 Direct-Q beats R1 in 5/8 cells with panel-median advantage `+0.5876%`, but this misses the preregistered >=1% dominance gate; verdict remains MIXED, not a proof that structured coordinates should be abandoned.

This result sharpens the v4.0 interpretation. v4.0 proved that the strict zero-sum constraint creates a **large oracle representation floor** after imperfect Level prediction, but v4.1 now shows that the excluded nonzero-mean component is **not reliably recoverable by the current learned delta mechanism**. Therefore “representation need” and “forecast-origin learnability” must be separated: the nonzero-mean remainder exists mathematically, yet granting a Local mean degree of freedom does not improve the panel when that degree is not predictable. The current bottleneck shifts from representation capacity to **observability / conditional predictability of the post-Level remainder mean**.

Do not rescue RCL by deeper delta networks, extra gates, market-specific routing, extra features or post-hoc calibration in the same branch without new evidence. Do not promote R1 into paper/core claims. The next legitimate discussion should first reconcile the strong v4.0 C2-RAW development behavior (7/8 positive vs Host, panel median ≈`+0.984%`) with the cleaner hierarchical v4.1 failure, using existing artifacts only, and determine which design change (joint vs frozen Level, raw residual history vs post-Level q-history, target parameterization, calibration, or information availability) actually produced the difference before authorizing another candidate.

## Residual-Complete Local deep design frozen — core alignment is a blocking prerequisite before any v4.1 scientific fit (2026-09-15)

A post-v4.0 source/math audit has materially refined the interpretation of the mechanism ladder. The structural result `CALIBRATED_ZERO_SUM=MATERIAL` remains valid, but the registered C2 candidate is not a clean implementation of the desired prediction-conditional principle. In `run_ladder.py`, C2 Local targets are built from `q=r-b_tilde*1`, yet the target-defining OOF `b_tilde` is not explicitly observed by the Local model; final inference uses a separately learned internal Level head; the coordinate target supervises that head toward `mean(q)` while `M/kappa/S` can reconstruct full `q`, so coordinate-perfect outputs generally cannot also make the final `L*1+z` reconstruct `r`; and C2 normalization/history remain tied to geometries that differ from the actual q target. Therefore `PREDICTION_CONDITIONAL_IMBALANCE=MIXED` is evidence about the registered C2 implementation, not a terminal rejection of prediction-conditional Local.

The new leading design is `docs/current/HCH_RESIDUAL_COMPLETE_LOCAL_DESIGN.md`. First train a standalone coarse Level predictor and freeze it. Use causal OOF Level predictions on TRAIN to define the actual leftover `q=r-b_tilde*1`; Local explicitly receives that Level prediction and uses history in the same post-Level q-space. Local is then made residual-complete through the unique conditional decomposition `q=delta*1+rho`, `rho=A(S+−S−)`, where `delta=mean(q)=b*-b_tilde`. Thus Local as a whole is allowed nonzero mean and can repair Level miss, while only the centered Shape component remains zero-sum. This removes the `M*kappa` product coupling, keeps the proven signed-simplex Shape machinery for the centered remainder, and avoids jointly free duplicate Level heads because the coarse Level predictor is frozen before Local target construction.

A matched development ladder is prepared, not executed, at `experiments/current/hch_residual_complete_local_20260915/`: `STACK_ZERO_SUM`, primary `RCL_ORTHOGONAL`, `UNTIED_Q`, and `DIRECT_Q`, all sharing one frozen Stage-1 Level, identical post-Level input/history and matched TRAIN support. Primary evaluation is raw output without dual-alpha rescue.

A subsequent source audit of the returned `RCL_PREPARATION_TESTS_PASS` found that preparation is **not yet method-complete**. The new `src/mvp/hch_residual_complete_local_v4_1/` currently implements only target/history helpers plus caller-side contract guards, while active `src/core` still encodes the previous alignment-safe exact-zero-sum method and explicitly forbids a daily-level/bias term. Therefore active core, paper-level method mathematics and the prepared experiment are not yet one implementation object. The blocking refactor authority is `docs/current/HCH_RCL_CORE_ALIGNMENT_REFACTOR_PLAN_20260915.md`; local-AI prompt is `experiments/current/hch_residual_complete_local_20260915/AI_CORE_ALIGNMENT_REFACTOR_PROMPT.md`. Before any scientific fit, the previous active core must be byte-preserved, active core must be rewritten around `CoarseLevelModel` + `ResidualCompleteLocalModel`, R1 must directly use active core rather than duplicate it under `src/mvp`, and six v4.0 semantic defects must be closed by structural tests rather than string/metadata guards. Required source-only terminal token: `RCL_V4_1_CORE_ALIGNMENT_READY_FOR_EXECUTION_REVIEW`.

No TEST/foreign/final/paper execution is authorized; no scientific fit is authorized until that token is returned and re-audited by the main window. `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` remains unchanged because this is still source/design work only.

## v4.0 mechanism ladder completed — zero-sum bottleneck confirmed, learnable imbalance/calibration/extreme gains remain heterogeneous (2026-09-15)

`HCH_V4_0_MECHANISM_LADDER_20260915` has completed after the finalization/audit repair. Independent verifier PASS: 16 required artifacts present, finalizer clean exit, 8 cells × 3 seeds × 4 neural candidates = **96/96 candidate fits**, identical `MECH_TRAIN=B2∪B3∪B4` target-day IDs across C0-MATCH/C2-RAW/C2-CAL/C3-DIRECT, chronological CAL/EVAL confirmed, warm-up+B1 excluded, and `test_label_read_count=0`. Terminal token: `HCH_V4_0_MECHANISM_LADDER_COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION`. Evidence root: `experiments/evidence/hch_v4_0_mechanism_ladder_20260915/`. The finalizer emits only NumPy empty-subset RuntimeWarnings (some registered extreme/failure subsets have no EVAL samples); there is no traceback and unavailable metrics remain NaN rather than being imputed.

Final registered labels: `CALIBRATED_ZERO_SUM=MATERIAL`; `DUAL_STATIC_CALIBRATION=MIXED`; `PREDICTION_CONDITIONAL_IMBALANCE=MIXED`; `CALIBRATION_BEFORE_RESIDUALIZATION=NOT_SUPPORTED`; `REPRESENTATION=MIXED`; `EXTREME_REPAIR=MIXED`.

The strongest scientific result is now structural: causal Level-only calibration does **not** collapse the zero-sum floor. Across all 8 cells calibrated median `|kappa|` remains `0.471..0.816` (panel median ≈`0.634`), versus raw `0.442..0.694`; 7/8 cells actually increase after calibration and the remaining SHAANXI/PatchTST only decreases slightly (`0.533→0.512`). Thus the earlier raw-kappa result was not merely Level scale error: strict zero-sum Local genuinely excludes a large nonzero-mean prediction-conditional residual component on this development panel.

However representational need is not the same as learnability. C2-CAL versus dual-calibrated C1-MATCH improves only 4/8 cells; registered gate is therefore `PREDICTION_CONDITIONAL_IMBALANCE=MIXED`. Cell gains are approximately: GANSU/LSTM `+2.357%`, GANSU/TimeMixer `-0.012%`, SHANDONG/TimeMixer `+0.535%`, SHANDONG/iTransformer `+0.061%`, SHAANXI/TimeMixer `-1.047%`, NINGXIA/LSTM `0%`, GANSU/PatchTST `+1.853%`, SHAANXI/PatchTST `-0.563%`. The single added bounded `kappa` degree therefore has real value in some hard cells but is not yet a stable universal correction mechanism.

The hypothesis that Level calibration should precede residualization is rejected on this panel: C2-CAL beats C2-RAW in only 3/8 cells and the panel-median gain is slightly negative (about `-0.019%`), so `CALIBRATION_BEFORE_RESIDUALIZATION=NOT_SUPPORTED`. Do not force calibrated residual targets in the next design merely because they are mathematically cleaner.

Dual static component calibration also weakens from the earlier six-cell diagnostic when tested on the matched 8-cell ladder: C1-MATCH versus C0-MATCH is positive in 4/8 cells with panel median ≈`+0.183%`, below the preregistered support threshold, hence `DUAL_STATIC_CALIBRATION=MIXED`. Component geometry remains heterogeneous: GANSU/LSTM and SHANDONG/TimeMixer are Level-dominant; GANSU/TimeMixer and SHANDONG/iTransformer complementary; SHAANXI/TimeMixer and NINGXIA/LSTM Local-dominant in the frozen six-cell Stage-0 map. Therefore dual scalars are useful as a calibration/control object but are not established as a universal headline contribution.

The unrestricted same-backbone direct residual control does not establish structured-coordinate underfit. C3-DIRECT beats C2-CAL in 4/8 cells, but its gains are modest where positive (`~0.05..0.84%`), whereas C2-CAL strongly beats Direct in GANSU/LSTM (`~13.79%`), SHANDONG/TimeMixer (`~4.37%`) and GANSU/PatchTST (`~1.89%`). Registered `REPRESENTATION=MIXED`: structured coordinates are not universally superior, but there is no evidence that they should be abandoned for an unrestricted residual vector.

Extreme repair is also heterogeneous (`MIXED`). Median combined-extreme C2-CAL vs C1 gains are approximately GANSU/LSTM `+2.14%`, GANSU/TimeMixer `+7.32%`, SHANDONG/TimeMixer `-3.22%`, SHANDONG/iTransformer `-3.33%`, SHAANXI/TimeMixer `+0.81%`, NINGXIA `0%`, GANSU/PatchTST `+15.34%`, SHAANXI/PatchTST `0%`. The strongest tail signal is therefore concentrated in GANSU, especially PatchTST, not panel-wide.

Overall EVAL context: C2-RAW is positive versus Host in 7/8 cells with panel-median Host gain ≈`+0.984%` (worst `-0.524%`); C2-CAL is positive in 4/8 with panel median ≈`+0.168%` but gives the strongest single-cell GANSU/LSTM gain (`~+12.44%` vs Host). These are development-slice results, not paper headline results. No frozen δ-Adapter/PIR/MDR/COSA artifact was sliceable onto this exact EVAL surface (`04_baselines/EVAL_SLICE_BASELINE_TABLE.csv` is all `NO_SLICEABLE_FROZEN_BASELINE_ARTIFACT_ADMITTED`), so this stage **cannot** claim competitiveness against those baselines.

Current bottleneck: zero-sum restriction is now scientifically confirmed as real, but the minimal prediction-conditional imbalance head is only conditionally learnable and gains are cell/extreme-regime dependent. The next method decision should therefore not return to hard zero-sum, but also should not blindly promote C2-CAL. Preserve the evidence that raw prediction-conditional residualization (C2-RAW) is broader than C2-CAL, and study why imbalance is beneficial in GANSU/SHANDONG but harmful or inactive in SHAANXI/NINGXIA before any final architecture freeze. No TEST/foreign/final/paper/core promotion is authorized by this completion alone.

## Previous plan — v4.0 pre-candidate component-geometry adjudication frozen — deconfound calibration from representation before any new network (2026-09-15)

A single comprehensive mechanism experiment is now frozen at `docs/current/HCH_COMPONENT_GEOMETRY_ADJUDICATION_DESIGN_20260915.md`, with executable protocol/prompt under `experiments/current/hch_component_geometry_adjudication_20260915/`. It deliberately performs **no new neural fits**. The goal is to exploit the already-generated OOF Level predictions and frozen v3.9 raw Level/Local components to answer four questions before any v4.0 candidate is trained: (1) after strictly causal nonnegative L1 calibration of raw OOF Level predictions, does the zero-sum MAE floor remain material; (2) does separate Level/Local calibration remain robust across three chronological CAL→EVAL cuts rather than only one 50/50 split; (3) are Level/Local failures primarily direction errors or scale errors under exact MAE alignment/safe-radius geometry; (4) across cells, is Level dominant, Local dominant, complementary, or should static correction abstain.

Track A uses only prior OOF blocks to fit `beta_L` and recomputes calibrated `d`, `kappa`, and zero-sum floor on B2..B4. Track B compares HOST / LEVEL_ONLY / LOCAL_ONLY / SHARED / SEPARATE across 1/3, 1/2 and 2/3 chronological VAL cuts. Track C uses only the pure mathematical functions in `src/core/safety.py` as offline diagnostics—never as a deployment controller—to separate correction-direction quality from calibration scale and to inspect frozen Tail/Normal masks. Track D produces a majority-vote component map and descriptive Level/Local head evidence. V2 TEST stays sealed (`test_label_read_count=0`), QINGHAI remains out, no Host/baseline rerun, no `src/core` edit, no gate/retrieval/new thresholds, and no paper update.

The next architecture decision is preregistered: only `CALIBRATED_ZERO_SUM_MATERIAL` can reopen prediction-conditional imbalance Local; if calibrated kappa collapses but dual-alpha is robust, retain zero-sum Local and fix calibration only. No new candidate is automatically authorized by this design. Because this is experiment design only, `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` is not advanced until local execution returns verified evidence.

## Predicted-Level Remainder diagnostic executed — raw zero-sum floor material, shared-alpha bottleneck supported, simple Level-remainder signal not detected (2026-09-15)

The registered diagnostic `HCH_PREDICTED_LEVEL_REMAINDER_DIAGNOSTIC_20260915` has completed with independent verifier PASS, 72/72 causal OOF fits and `test_label_read_count=0`. Final labels are: `SIMPLE_SIGNAL_NOT_DETECTED`, `ZERO_SUM_RESTRICTION_MATERIAL`, and `SHARED_ALPHA_BOTTLENECK_SUPPORTED`; terminal token `HCH_PREDICTED_LEVEL_REMAINDER_DIAGNOSTIC_COMPLETE_FOR_ADJUDICATION`. Evidence root: `experiments/evidence/hch_predicted_level_remainder_diagnostic_20260915/`; adjudication: `docs/current/HCH_PREDICTED_LEVEL_REMAINDER_POSTEXECUTION_ADJUDICATION_20260915.md`.

The Level remainder result is stronger than merely weak: fixed causal Ridge is worse than ZERO in all 6 cells (`-33.77% .. -5.34%`, panel median about `-16.16%`), while ACF(1)/ACF(7) panel medians are near zero. This means the tested simple Host-statistics/calendar/lagged-remainder feature set does not expose stable causal predictability of `d=b*-b_tilde`; it does **not** prove theoretical unpredictability.

Raw zero-sum restriction is material in all 6 cells. Cell-median `|kappa|` ranges `0.442..0.694` with panel median about `0.600`; roughly `88%..93%` of OOF days have `|kappa|>0.1`. Median raw zero-sum MAE floors are about `31..49` price units against remainder MAE medians about `63..84`, so after the **raw uncalibrated** OOF Level prediction a zero-sum Local is structurally forbidden from correcting a large part of the remaining error. However this evidence is conditional on raw `b_tilde`: Track D independently shows that Level and Local require different calibration, so part of the large raw kappa may be Level scale/calibration error. Before any untied-amplitude training, recompute kappa after strictly causal Level-only scalar calibration using the already-persisted OOF predictions; no new neural fits are needed for that adjudication.

Shared alpha is supported as a real bottleneck under the preregistered CAL/EVAL test: separate Level/Local calibration improves 4/6 cells, panel median EVAL gain `+0.874%`, worst degradation `-0.178%`. More importantly, the joint L1 optimum frequently zeros one component (GANSU/LSTM keeps Level only; GANSU/TimeMixer keeps Local only; SHANDONG/TimeMixer keeps Level only; SHANDONG/iTransformer nearly Local only; SHAANXI uses both; NINGXIA uses neither), showing component-direction heterogeneity rather than a single common scale mismatch. A future Level+Local candidate should not retain one shared scalar alpha; two static nonnegative component scalars are now the minimal supported calibration object, not an instance gate/safety mechanism.

No untied Local, dual-alpha candidate, TEST/foreign/final execution, safety/gate/retrieval, or `src/core` change is automatically authorized by this diagnostic. The next scientific question is whether the zero-sum floor remains material **after** causal Level-only calibration; only then should a prediction-conditional untied Local be considered.
