## HCH Level–Local v3.9 pilot closed — hard zero-sum Local is no longer assumed sufficient under learned Level error (2026-09-15)

The authorized `HCH_LEVEL_LOCAL_V3_9_VAL_PILOT_20260915` has completed and independently verified with terminal token `HCH_LEVEL_LOCAL_V3_9_NOT_SUPPORTED_STOP_FOR_ADJUDICATION`. Exactly 18/18 TRAIN/VAL fits ran on the same six v3.8 cells and seeds 7/17/37; V2 TEST target read count remained 0 and the Local zero-sum invariant passed. Cell-median gains vs Host / frozen M0 were: GANSU/LSTM `+4.929% / +5.070%`; GANSU/TimeMixer `+0.013% / -1.251%`; SHANDONG/TimeMixer `+0.710% / +1.178%`; SHANDONG/iTransformer `+0.534% / -0.770%`; SHAANXI/TimeMixer `+0.657% / -1.877%`; NINGXIA/LSTM `0.000% / +1.446%`. Gate outcome: 6/6 non-worse than Host, only 3/6 better than M0, and Level coordinate only 2/6 better than zero reference. The NINGXIA apparent M0 win is abstention rather than candidate improvement: all three seeds have `alpha_0=0`; GANSU/TimeMixer also has median `alpha_0≈0.080`, so its correction is nearly shut off.

The previous claim that adding Level makes hard-zero-sum Local essentially free of representational concern is now narrowed. The oracle identity `r=b*1+A*(S+*-S-*)` remains mathematically exact **only when the true target-dependent `b*=mean(r)` is known**. With a learned `b_hat`, any hard-zero-sum Local cannot compensate Level prediction error: `mean(r-alpha(b_hat 1+z_hat))=b*-alpha b_hat` for `1^T z_hat=0`, hence daily MAE has lower bound `|b*-alpha b_hat|`. Because Level is weak in this pilot, this is an actual architectural bottleneck, not merely a theoretical detail. Local amplitude repair is also not established as solved: centered-amplitude gap improves only 2/6 cells, worsens 3/6, and ties 1/6; centered-shape diagnostics are somewhat better but modest. One global `alpha_0` further couples Level and Local scaling strongly (seed-median alpha by cell ranges from `0` to `1.630`).

A more rigorous successor hypothesis is now **predicted-Level residualization**, not naive untied amplitudes. First produce chronological/out-of-fold TRAIN Level predictions `b_tilde`; then define the Local target from the residual the Level model actually leaves, `q*=r-b_tilde 1`, and decompose it exactly as `q*=A+*S+*-A-*S-*`. This gives the exact identity `A+*-A-* = H(b*-b_tilde)`: signed-mass imbalance has a precise meaning as the remaining daily-mean error of the Level predictor rather than an unconstrained duplicate Level head. Recommended parameterization is total mass plus imbalance ratio `kappa=(A+-A-)/(A++A-)`, where `kappa=0` recovers the current zero-sum model. No new training is yet authorized. Before any v4.0 protocol, run only read-only diagnostics for Level-residual observability, the distribution of `kappa*` under out-of-fold/predicted Level, and whether optimal scalar calibration differs materially between Level, Local and the full correction. A new closest-work collision must also be carried forward: Fu, Feng & Niu's 2026 Neurocomputing DARC paper already performs structured dual-axis residual decomposition/correction across time series tasks, so future novelty cannot be framed merely as "structured residual decomposition and separate correction". Controlling note: `docs/current/HCH_V3_9_POSTPILOT_MATH_ADJUDICATION_20260915.md`.

## HCH Level–Local v3.9 design frozen — Gain removed, Local retained as amplitude + shape repair (2026-09-15)

The v3.8 Full-LGR pilot has now produced a narrower design decision: **remove the explicit Host-aligned Gain coordinate from the leading route, but do not remove amplitude repair**. The next candidate is `Level + Local`, prepared at `docs/current/HCH_LEVEL_LOCAL_V3_9_DESIGN_20260915.md` with an execution protocol at `experiments/current/hch_level_local_v3_9_pilot_20260915/PROTOCOL.md` and a short local-AI prompt at `AI_EXECUTION_PROMPT.md`. ChatGPT will not execute this pilot; the local executor is responsible for implementation, fitting and verification.

The mathematical point is decisive. For `r = y - yhat_H`, set `b*=mean(r)` and `rho*=r-b*1`. Then `sum rho*=0`, so `rho*=A*(S+ - S-)` exactly with `A=||rho||_1/2`. Thus the existing tied positive/negative Local mass is **not** an expressivity defect once Level is present. Local's scalar `A` is the amplitude of the centered correction and `S+/S-` allocates that amplitude over the day. A Host curve whose daily excursion is too weak/strong is therefore a Local residual pattern after mean removal; it does not require a separate Gain head.

Do **not** untie `A+` and `A-`: their difference would reintroduce a nonzero-sum degree of freedom and make Local compete with the explicit Level coordinate. Do not rescue the stopped Gain head with MLP/attention/TCN/Transformer/gate. The v3.8 result rejects the explicit Host-aligned scalar Gain coordinate as an independently supported object, not amplitude correction itself.

v3.9 is intentionally the smallest candidate: `c_hat = b_hat*1 + A_hat(S_hat+ - S_hat-)`, one signed Level scalar, the existing Local Shape+mass machinery, one VAL `alpha_0`, no safety/gate/retrieval, no `src/core` edit, same six v3.8 cells and seeds 7/17/37. The pilot adds only read-only amplitude diagnostics (centered amplitude gap, centered-shape MAE, Level residual, Local zero-sum invariant) so the experiment can directly answer whether Local is actually repairing amplitude rather than merely shifting mass.

This is a **design decision, not an experiment result**. `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` is not advanced until the local executor returns a verified scientific run. V2 TEST and foreign/final data remain closed.

## HCH-LGR v3.8 pilot executed — Full LGR not yet supported; Gain head unsupported (2026-09-15)

The authorized isolated implementation `src/mvp/hch_lgr_v3_8/` and TRAIN/VAL feasibility pilot have now been executed. Controlling design/protocol/prompt: `docs/current/HCH_LGR_V3_8_IMPLEMENTATION_DESIGN_20260915.md`, `experiments/current/hch_lgr_v3_8_pilot_20260915/PROTOCOL.md`, `experiments/current/hch_lgr_v3_8_pilot_20260915/AI_EXECUTION_PROMPT.md`. `src/core` was not intentionally modified by this work. The pilot used 6 cells × seeds 7/17/37 = 18 fits, CPU process-parallel, V2 frozen TRAIN/VAL only, and reported TEST target read count 0. QINGHAI was not included.

Cell medians on VAL: GANSU/LSTM `+5.323%` vs Host and `+5.741%` vs M0; GANSU/TimeMixer `0.000%` vs Host and `−1.331%` vs M0; SHANDONG/TimeMixer `+1.462%` and `+2.094%`; SHANDONG/iTransformer `+0.521%` and `+1.353%`; SHAANXI/TimeMixer `+0.305%` and `−2.276%`; NINGXIA/LSTM `0.000%` and `+1.446%`. Across six cells LGR is non-worse than Host 6/6 and improves M0 4/6, but worst degradation vs M0 is `−2.276%`, failing the registered ≤2% stability gate.

Mechanism result is more informative than the aggregate: Level coordinate sanity passes only 3/6 cells; Gain coordinate sanity passes **0/6**; local-mass error is lower than its zero-local reference in 6/6. Thus the existing Local branch remains the strongest learned object, Level has partial signal, and the current Host-aligned Gain target/head has no support in this pilot. Two failed cells also show non-positive correction/residual alignment and one calibrates `alpha_0=0`, indicating candidate-direction failure rather than under-correction.

Therefore the registered Full-LGR pilot is **stopped**. No safety/gate/retrieval rescue, no Gain-network expansion, no extra variants, no V2 TEST, no foreign/final data. The scientifically interesting next question is whether the smallest `Level + Local` candidate is more coherent than Full LGR; that is a new design decision and requires a fresh authorization, not an automatic follow-up experiment.

Evidence/report: `experiments/evidence/hch_lgr_v3_8_pilot_20260915/PILOT_REPORT.md`, `RESULTS_MEDIAN.csv`. Important evidence boundary: this was an informative stdout-only feasibility execution; 18 per-seed checkpoints/raw prediction artifacts were not persisted, so it is **not paper-verified evidence**. Do not put these numbers into the paper as final results.

Literature boundary also tightened: Theil-style bias/scale/covariance decomposition and Hodson et al.'s bias–distribution–sequence decomposition already establish forecast-error decomposition, including timing/phase as sequence error. Therefore LGR cannot claim decomposition itself as novel. The possible novelty remains the narrower Host-relative correction coordinate system plus signed-simplex local tail repair; closest-work collision audit remains required.

Canonical research state for the HCH electricity-price post-processing project.
Last updated: 2026-09-15

## LGR concrete design frozen at paper/design level — sequential targets, parallel three-head prediction, two-term loss (2026-09-15)

The Level–Gain–Redistribution direction has moved from conceptual decomposition to a concrete minimal architecture design. Controlling design authority remains `docs/current/HCH_LEVEL_GAIN_REDISTRIBUTION_DESIGN.md`. The scientific principle is now explicit: **humans may first notice local peak/valley misalignment, but the model must not execute a local-first serial correction pipeline.** TRAIN targets are constructed sequentially and identifiably as `Level -> Gain -> Local`, while one shared network predicts all coordinates in parallel to avoid competing explanations and error propagation.

The frozen design candidate is `c_hat = b_hat*1 + g_hat*u + A_hat_rho(S_hat_rho+ - S_hat_rho-)`, where `u = yhat_H - mean(yhat_H)1`. The existing shared stem + Shape GRU + magnitude-sensitive GRU are retained. Level and Gain add only two signed affine scalar readouts from the existing magnitude latent (implementation may use one 2-output linear layer); Local fully reuses the existing Shape+shared-mass mechanism but is trained against the zero-sum local target `rho*`, not the whole residual. Gain is deterministically masked on near-flat Host days; no TCN, Transformer, retrieval, router, learned gate, market expert or componentwise confidence selector is part of the leading design.

The training objective is deliberately simplified to two top-level terms only: `L = L_rec + L_coord`. `L_rec` is normalized full-residual MAE of the synthesized correction. `L_coord` is the equal-weight average of normalized Level MAE, Gain MAE, local-mass MAE, and signed-Shape W1 over active/identifiable coordinates. All target scales are TRAIN-only robust statistics; no per-head loss-weight sweep is part of the first design. After candidate freeze, retain exactly one nonnegative VAL scalar `alpha_0` on the full synthesized correction; no `alpha_L/alpha_G/alpha_R` or learned head gates.

The user explicitly authorized this design to be written into `paper/**` and `paper/current/**` for the current conversation. The manuscript-facing Introduction/Related Work/Methodology/Experiments/Discussion/Conclusion now distinguish the **proposed but not yet freshly validated full LGR** from the **already-validated Local-only predecessor M0**. Existing China-5 M0 numbers are retained only as Local-branch evidence, representation-gap motivation and future ablation reference; they must not be described as full-LGR performance. Paper Figure 1 planning is updated to show three real error archetypes (Level/Gain/Local) plus residual-energy composition; Figure 2 is the shared-backbone parallel-head LGR architecture. No new scientific experiment has been run, so `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` remains unchanged.

## Leading candidate-quality redesign — Level + Host-aligned Gain + local signed redistribution (2026-09-15)

Controlling discussion note: `docs/current/HCH_LEVEL_GAIN_REDISTRIBUTION_DESIGN.md`. The new bottleneck is candidate representation, not safety. Current M0 `c=A(S+−S−)` is reinterpreted as a model for the **local zero-sum residual component only**, rather than the whole Host residual. For each day define centered Host curve `u = yhat_H - mean(yhat_H)1`, daily level `b*=mean(r)`, Host-aligned gain `g*=<r-b*1,u>/||u||^2`, and local residual `rho*=r-b*1-g*u`. Then `r=b*1+g*u+rho*`, with `sum rho*=0` and `<rho*,u>=0` when the gain coordinate is active. This gives three interpretable EPF error modes: whole-day offset, globally under/over-amplified Host daily excursion, and local timing/shape/extreme error.

A key mathematical consequence is now explicit: because `rho*` is zero-sum, its positive and negative masses are **exactly equal**, so `rho*=A_rho(S_rho+−S_rho−)` with one shared nonnegative local mass. This turns the existing tied/shared Amplitude from an empirical surviving restriction into a principled representation **after** Level and Gain are removed. The proposed minimal extension therefore needs only two extra signed scalars (`b`, `g`) from the existing scale-sensitive latent; no new GRU/Transformer/retrieval/gate is required. Safety remains supplementary and is not part of this redesign.

Literature audit boundary: additive/multiplicative forecast calibration (MOS/EVMOS), mean/scale normalization/restoration (RevIN, Non-stationary Transformers, Dish-TS), amplitude/phase or shape/timing decomposition (functional-data literature, DILATE), and positional/structural/residual forecast-error decomposition in meteorology already exist. Electricity-price work also contains spike-specific two-stage calibration and residual post-processing. Therefore no claim of “first level/scale/shape decomposition” is allowed. The potentially differentiated object is narrower: an identifiable **Host-relative residual correction coordinate system** for a frozen day-ahead electricity-price forecaster, with a signed-simplex local repair targeted at difficult/tail errors. No novelty-first claim is frozen yet.

A read-only no-new-model China-5 diagnostic has now been completed on the already-frozen V2 Host prediction/actual arrays; no source/model/checkpoint changed. The single-lag narrative is **not** supported as a universal problem statement: centered Host-vs-actual alignment over candidate shifts from -3 to +3 hours gives a non-zero best shift on average 41.5% of VAL days and 43.4% of TEST days, while the median optimal shift remains zero in most cells. On high-error days the non-zero fraction rises to about 61.5% in VAL and 54.6% in TEST, so lag/temporal misalignment is a real difficult-instance subtype rather than the whole problem.

The orthogonal three-component residual hypothesis is substantially better supported. Averaging per-day L2 energy shares over the 20 market×Host cells gives VAL `29.3% Level / 15.7% Gain / 55.0% Local` and TEST `29.6% / 17.1% / 53.3%`, showing strong VAL/TEST stability and that all three components are non-negligible. TEST market-average shares are approximately GANSU `35.2/13.2/51.6`, SHANDONG `25.4/30.7/43.9`, SHAANXI `24.1/15.9/60.0`, NINGXIA `36.5/14.7/48.8`, QINGHAI `26.9/10.8/62.4` percent. Preferred problem framing is therefore **structured frozen-Host residual correction with identifiable Level, Host-aligned Gain and Local redistribution modes**; lag/phase is one motivating Local pattern, not the top-level definition. No neural redesign is authorized yet; the next design discussion should decide the minimal module/ablation around these three coordinates. `paper/**` remains untouched because it requires explicit user authorization.

## Post-execution PIR pilot audit — RLR closed; RRC/LCR verdicts suspended pending one fidelity replay (2026-09-14)

Controlling audit: `docs/current/HCH_PIR_MINIMAL_PROBE_POSTEXECUTION_AUDIT_20260914.md`. The executor's `HCH_PIR_MINIMAL_PROBE_VERIFIED` token remains valid for evidence integrity (13/13, TEST read count 0, 24 fits, unchanged core/Host/baseline artifacts), but a source-to-protocol audit found implementation gaps not covered by that verifier. RRC violated its registered scale contract: protocol requires `retrieved_residual_reference = tilde_r / TRAIN residual scale`, while `pilot_runner.make_batch()` appends raw `tilde_r` to the already-canonicalized base token. RRC and LCR also instantiate the model **before** calling `torch.manual_seed(seed)`, so registered seeds 7/17/37 do not control parameter initialization. Required retrieval diagnostics are incomplete as well: Pearson is present, but registered Spearman and signed-Shape W1 are absent.

RLR has an additional seed ambiguity: it is labelled deterministic/no-seed but the runner implicitly uses the last frozen M0 stream (`seed=37`) while its reference is a three-seed cell median. A read-only replay over all three frozen M0 streams removes that ambiguity and still fails clearly: relative Overall changes become GANSU/TimeMixer `+0.739%`, SHAANXI/PatchTST `-0.034%`, NINGXIA/TimeMixer `-6.053%`, SHANDONG/PatchTST control `-0.109%`. Therefore **RLR is scientifically closed**; fixed K=10 Host-profile-cosine residual-level retrieval is not the explanation for PIR's strength.

Retrieval diagnostics explain the failure: average top-10 Host-profile cosine is high (`0.903..0.965`), but retrieved-level vs realised-level Pearson is weak/wrong-sign (`-0.437..+0.209`) and retrieved-residual trajectory correlation is only `-0.011..+0.277`. Similar Host forecast shape is therefore not similar Host residual geometry. PIR's advantage cannot be reduced to naive nearest-neighbour retrieval.

RRC and LCR scientific verdicts are **suspended**, not promoted. RRC's negative result is not a faithful test of the registered scaled-channel object. LCR shows conditional signal (challenge changes `-1.755/+2.599/+0.755%`, control `-4.982%`; two challenge cells improve but GANSU/SHANDONG are harmed), but its exact aggregate is invalidated by the seed-initialization defect. Authorize exactly one bounded implementation-fidelity replay of RRC/LCR only: seed before model construction; apply the registered RRC residual scaling; emit missing Spearman/signed-Shape-W1 diagnostics; otherwise preserve the same four cells, TRAIN/CAL/EVAL identities, K=10, seeds, optimizer, loss, epochs, patience and support gates. No V2 TEST, foreign/final data, RLR rerun, combined variant, gate, retrieval sweep or core edit. If neither passes after this replay, close the PIR-inspired minimal-adaptation route and return to frozen M0 for fresh external tail-repair evaluation.

## PIR mechanism audit opens one isolated TRAIN+VAL candidate-quality probe; core and V2 TEST remain frozen (2026-09-14)

Controlling design: `docs/current/HCH_PIR_MECHANISM_AUDIT_AND_MINIMAL_ADAPTATION_20260914.md`; active pilot stage: `experiments/current/hch_pir_inspired_minimal_probe_20260914/PROTOCOL.md`. The V2 result established that safety is not the main bottleneck: M0 beats δ-Adapter 18/20 but PIR only 3/10 on legal Overall-MAE overlaps, while M0 is much stronger in the intended difficult region (Tail 7/10 and Lower-tail 7/10 vs PIR). A focused PIR paper/code audit identifies three mechanisms: failure identification, local contextual revision and global historical retrieval. The first is deliberately not copied because it collides conceptually with the already-rejected learned Proposal Verification/Gate; the local information inventory already overlaps substantially with our Host+forecast-known-feature stem; the largest credible missing object is global historical reference information plus freedom to correct daily level/scale.

A new mathematical blind spot is now explicit. Current M0 enforces `c=A(S+−S−)` and therefore `sum_h c_h=0`; hence for every day `mean_h(r−alpha c)=mean_h(r)` exactly, and by Jensen the repaired daily MAE is lower-bounded by the absolute Host daily mean residual. PIR's local/global revision has no such zero-sum restriction and the PIR paper explicitly shows global retrieval correcting future scale. This gives a concrete explanation for the V2 pattern "strong Tail repair but weaker Overall/Normal than PIR" that does not reduce to model size.

Exactly three minimal directions are registered **outside `src/core`**: (A) `RLR` Residual-Level Retrieval, a deterministic K=10 TRAIN-only analogue that adds one retrieved residual-level scalar to frozen M0 and requires no neural retraining; (B) `RRC` Retrieved Residual Reference Channel, one synthetic H-length retrieved residual channel passed through the existing shared stem while preserving the zero-sum candidate; (C) `LCR` Level-Centered Redistribution, adding exactly one signed level scalar head and training Shape/Amplitude on median-centered residuals. No Transformer, learned uncertainty/gate, router, retrieval hyperparameter sweep or combined variant is permitted in this pilot.

The pilot is development-only and **must not read V2 TEST**. Fixed cells are `GANSU_DA/TimeMixer`, `SHAANXI_DA/PatchTST`, `NINGXIA_DA/TimeMixer` (three PIR-gap challenge cells) plus `SHANDONG_DA/PatchTST` (control where M0 already beats PIR). It uses frozen V2 TRAIN and splits VAL chronologically into PILOT_CAL/PILOT_EVAL; reference M0 is replayed from existing TRAIN-fitted checkpoints, RRC/LCR each run 4×3=12 fits at seeds 7/17/37 under the unchanged candidate optimizer/training schedule, and RLR is deterministic. Total new neural fits are 24. Any passing variant is only `SUPPORTED_FOR_FRESH_EXTERNAL_TEST`; no combination or core promotion is automatic. `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` is unchanged until the pilot actually runs.

## Independent post-V2 review — tail-repair evidence is stronger than Overall-SOTA evidence; no safety rescue (2026-09-13)

Independent review: `docs/current/HCH_V2_INDEPENDENT_RESULT_REVIEW_AND_NEXT_DECISION_20260913.md`. The verified V2 execution is accepted subject to the existing historical-exposure caveat; current `src/core` still re-hashes to `9B278C5A22C8E88D66A0D6AAD8347C55799BF8A4152484DEDF1E0E8EC695D7AB`, and the active-core suite independently re-runs `136 passed`. The missing-target continuation's 12/12 checker lives in the same re-aggregation module rather than a wholly independent second implementation, so provenance claims must state that accurately; direct recomputation from final exports confirms the reported result shape.

Safety is no longer the active bottleneck. M2/Shape-weighted safety remains deleted: it is functionally non-binding relative to M1 in all recorded cell×seed summaries. M1 is retained only as an optional prequential stabilizer because it reduces the worst Overall negative transfer from `-0.652%` to `-0.054%` while leaving the panel median unchanged; it improves 6 cells, worsens 10, ties 4, and reduces median Tail gain from about `+7.276%` to `+4.429%`. **Do not spend another V2 round optimizing safety.** A shrink-only controller cannot create missing residual information.

The main candidate M0 is broad but not Overall-SOTA against PIR: median Overall gain `+1.656%`, 17/20 positive, M0 beats delta-Adapter 18/20 but PIR only 3/10 legal overlaps and the per-cell best of delta/PIR only 13/20. However its difficult-region evidence is materially stronger: against PIR it wins Tail-MAE 7/10, Lower-tail 7/10 and Negative-price 2/2 defined overlaps; against delta-Adapter it wins Tail 15/20, Lower-tail 16/20, Negative-price 3/4 defined cells and Extreme-day 10/12 defined cells. The cleaner paper direction is therefore **frozen-Host extreme/tail residual repair**, not universal Overall-MAE postprocessing superiority.

QINGHAI must not be removed post hoc to improve the table: under V2 M0 is positive on all four QINGHAI Hosts, so the old 4/4 low-shot failure is protocol-dependent historical motivation rather than a reason for exclusion. Any dataset exclusion must rest on an independent publication/data contract fixed before the next comparison.

Next-step fork: if broad Overall superiority to the strongest baseline is mandatory, stop before foreign final evaluation and design a genuinely new candidate-quality idea on a fresh evaluation surface; safety is not the lever. If the paper is positioned around difficult-region/tail repair, freeze M0 as the primary method, keep M1 only as a setting-separated deployment stabilizer, and proceed to fresh foreign/public markets with Tail/upper-lower/negative/extreme metrics preregistered as primary targets and Overall-MAE as a guardrail.

## V2 scientific adjudication — modest broad repair, uniform shrinkage supported only as stabilizer, Shape-weighted safety deleted (2026-09-13)

Human interpretation is frozen at `docs/current/HCH_V2_RESULT_ADJUDICATION_20260913.md`. Under the verified full-eligible 70/10/20 benchmark, M0 has median Overall-MAE gain **+1.656%**, improves **17/20** Hosts and is non-worse in **19/20**; M1 has median **+1.652%**, also 17/20 positive and 19/20 non-worse, but reduces the worst M0 cell from `−0.652%` (SHAANXI_DA/TimeMixer) to `−0.054%`. The old low-shot `+8.434%` median gain is therefore not representative of the standard benchmark budget.

The old QINGHAI 4/4 catastrophe does not reproduce: M0 is positive on all four QINGHAI Hosts (`+0.021/+0.580/+3.468/+2.625%`), and M1/M2 remain positive (`+0.031/+1.453/+3.470/+2.565%`). QINGHAI is no longer treated as intrinsically unlearnable; the earlier failure was strongly protocol/regime dependent.

Uniform safety M1 is supported only as a **recent-history overall-MAE harm-reduction/shrinkage mechanism**, not as an accuracy booster: relative to M0 it improves 6 cells, worsens 10 and ties 4, with essentially unchanged panel median. M2 is **not supported**: M1 and M2 are numerically identical in all 20 cell medians, and `lambda_final_M1 == lambda_final_M2` in every cell×seed summary, so Shape-weighted `lambda_S` never becomes a binding decision constraint. Under the project's delete-if-no-effect rule, Shape-weighted safety is removed from the leading method; do not retune/rescue it on V2.

Baseline interpretation also changes. M0 beats delta-Adapter in **18/20**, but beats PIR in only **3/10** legal overlaps; PIR's median gain on its legal PatchTST/TimeMixer cells is **+5.475%** (8/10 positive), so no broad superiority-to-PIR claim is allowed. In the prequential setting COSA improves 20/20 Hosts with median gain about **+0.815%**, while M1/M2 beat COSA in **15/20**. M0 median Tail-MAE gain is about **+7.276%**, M1/M2 about **+4.429%**, but max normal-region relative harm remains about **+18%**; therefore “Safety” must remain narrowly defined as overall-MAE causal shrinkage, not subgroup/general no-harm.

Leading supported structure after V2: offline-static M0 = minimal candidate `c=A(S+−S−)` + VAL `alpha_0`; optional prequential M1 = `Host + min(alpha_0,lambda_U)c`. M2 / Shape-weighted safety is no longer an active component. No V2 rescue/tuning experiment is authorized; next discussion should focus on paper positioning and fresh external/future-time confirmation.

## Missing-target adjudication resolved — preserve V2 split, score 329/330 Shandong TEST days, no retraining (2026-09-13)

Human adjudication is frozen at `docs/current/HCH_V2_MISSING_TARGET_ADJUDICATION_20260913.md`. This day is the **last** Shandong TEST day (index 329 of 330).

The adjudicated policy is **not** to change or recompute the 70/10/20 split. V2 TEST membership remains frozen at 330 Shandong days, but metric scorability is target-availability based: a day contributes to target-dependent metrics iff its entire H=24 target vector is finite. Therefore Shandong reports `n_test_days=330`, `n_scored_test_days=329`, `n_unscorable_missing_target_days=1`, with `2026-07-17` retained in raw provenance as `NOT_SCORABLE_MISSING_TARGET / TARGET_VECTOR_NONFINITE_24_OF_24`. No target is imputed, no replacement day is pulled in, and no model-specific mask is allowed. All methods use the identical market-level scorable-day mask.

Because the missing outcome is on the final TEST day, no later COSA/M1/M2 prediction can depend on its reveal/update. Thus **no Host, baseline or candidate retraining and no sequential inference replay are scientifically required**. The legitimate continuation is metric/verifier re-aggregation from the already-frozen Phase-B raw artifacts, preserving all split/checkpoint/candidate/prediction hashes for scored days. The continuation prompt is `experiments/current/hch_china5_common_benchmark_701020_full_v2/AI_MISSING_TARGET_ADJUDICATION_CONTINUATION_PROMPT.md`. It is a scoring-contract repair only; `src/core`, W=7, alpha, seeds, losses, architecture and split remain unchanged. Any final V2 row must carry `data_adjudication = V2_MISSING_TARGET_SCORABILITY_ADJUDICATION_20260913` plus registered/scored/unscorable TEST-day counts.

This is still a post-TEST data-quality amendment, so the existing `COMMON_BENCHMARK_TEST_WITH_HISTORICAL_EXPOSURE_CAVEAT` remains. V2 can become the standardized development/common-benchmark result after independent re-verification, but not a claim of untouched final confirmation.

## Baseline split/data-budget audit changes interpretation; further market expansion paused (2026-09-12)

Controlling audit: `docs/current/BASELINE_SPLIT_PROTOCOL_LITERATURE_AUDIT_20260912.md`. A paper/code-level review of δ-Adapter, PIR, COSA, UEC-STD, OMPB, ICDE forecasting comparisons, standard Time-Series-Library/PatchTST splits, and day-ahead electricity-price forecasting practice finds a **material split/sample-budget confound** in the current strict matrices. The current `HOST_TRAIN≈45% / HOST_VAL≈5% / POST_TRAIN≈15% / DEV_EVAL≈5% / PROTECTED_FINAL≈30%` protocol is valid as a deliberately hard **frozen-Host, temporally disjoint, low-shot post-deployment transfer stress test**, but it is not a neutral paper-native baseline protocol and is not the dominant EPF deployment protocol.

Key evidence: δ-Adapter and PIR train their revision/adaptation machinery on the ordinary benchmark training split (generic ~70%, ETT fixed ~60%) with validation selection and held-out test; COSA adapts over the full held-out test stream; UEC-STD uses 70% backbone training + 10% held-out validation-derived correction data + 20% test and explicitly shows a 5%-validation corrector can still work; OMPB is a separate source→target online-shift problem. ICDE LiPFormer/FOCUS/MUSE-Net follow the usual comparison rule: **one common benchmark split for all compared methods, with method configs inherited from original work**, rather than starving each baseline with a new method-specific data partition. Mainstream EPF practice uses long out-of-sample windows and rolling/daily recalibration; the Lago/epftoolbox benchmark uses a two-year test and recommends at least one year for meaningful EPF evaluation.

Therefore existing matrix numbers remain frozen and valid **only for `HARD_FROZEN_LOW_SHOT_TRANSFER`**. They must not currently be used alone to claim that PIR/δ-Adapter are intrinsically non-transferable to electricity-price residuals or that market residual geometry is the sole cause of harm. The new bottleneck is to adjudicate split/data budget on a small representative panel before adding more markets or evaluating the proposed method. Recommended comparison settings are: (A) existing hard low-shot transfer, reused; (B) a common literature-standard benchmark split with each baseline’s official data-use semantics; and (C) rolling/expanding EPF deployment with a long chronological test. **Further market expansion is paused until this adjudication is resolved.**

## Alignment-safe core history-contract patch executed — evidence-bank trust boundary hardened, still zero scientific runs (2026-09-12)

The bounded audit patch `docs/current/HCH_ALIGNMENT_SAFE_CORE_HISTORY_PATCH_PROMPT_20260912.md` has been **executed**. It touches exactly two files: `src/core/history.py` and `experiments/foundation/tests/test_core_safety.py`. It is **source/audit work only** — no scientific DEV run, no `PROTECTED_FINAL` read, no `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` entry.

**Closing the two bypasses the independent review found.** One internal validator now judges every record at every door — live `append_candidate`/`attach_residual`, `select_warm_start`/`from_warm_start`, and `from_state` — requiring a non-empty delivery ID, legal prequential/OOF provenance, `candidate_created_at < ordinal`, and, for completed records, `revealed_at is not None`, `revealed_at >= ordinal`, `revealed_at > candidate_created_at`; a pending record must carry neither residual nor reveal time. Nothing is repaired in place: a malformed declared-legal record raises. Warm start additionally requires unique delivery IDs and strictly increasing ordinals over the selected window, and restore additionally requires every active record to be present in the persisted seen-ID ledger (which stays a superset, so evicted deliveries remain non-replayable), strictly chronological active ordinals, and no more than seven completed records. The review's exact adversarial case — seven records with legal provenance, legal candidate timing, a non-null residual and `revealed_at = 0` for ordinals 10..16 — used to build a ready bank and is now refused.

**Evidence is owned, not aliased.** Tensors entering the bank become `detach().clone().cpu()` snapshots and every public surface (`records`, `pending`, `completed`, `as_arrays`) hands back copies, so a caller that mutates a supplied or retrieved tensor in place can no longer rewrite evidence the controller has already scored, and the bank retains no autograd graph or caller storage.

**Verification.** Active-core subset `136 passed` (was 120; +16 adversarial tests). Full foundation suite `10 failed, 160 passed` — the identical ten pre-existing legacy-manifest/evidence paths, reported not fixed. The archived 20-file core still re-hashes to `0c53faab…bb99` and both frozen evidence roots are unchanged (`3de082de…8f57`, `7b1f5c04…dcb1`). `safety.py` safe-ray mathematics, `W=7`, candidate architecture, losses, calibration and mask semantics are byte-unchanged from the refactor commit. All sixteen new tests were confirmed non-vacuous: fifteen of them fail against the pre-patch `history.py`.

**This closes the review's experiment-readiness blocker but authorizes nothing.** Terminal token `ALIGNMENT_SAFE_CORE_READY_FOR_M0_M1_M2_EXPERIMENT_DESIGN` means the core may now be *designed against*; it is not authorization to run M0/M1/M2, and the closed signed-mass verdict remains untouched and unrescued.

## Alignment-safe core independent review — mathematics/source PASS, one history-contract patch required before experiment design (2026-09-12)

Independent human audit is recorded at `docs/current/HCH_ALIGNMENT_SAFE_CORE_REVIEW_20260912.md`. The refactor is scientifically faithful at the model/mathematics level: the archived 20-file core independently re-hashes to `0c53faab…bb99`; the active core subset independently re-runs at **120/120 PASS**; a direct parameter mapping from the archived closed `FROZEN_SMALLEST` model to the new minimal candidate gives exact Shape/Amplitude outputs and max correction discrepancy only `1.57e-09`; and an independent 1000-ray MAE root oracle gives **0 mismatches** with worst absolute error `5.77e-15`. The active path is the **empty switch vector** plus a non-trainable controller: one shared MLP stem, Shape GRU32 → `S±`, Amplitude GRU32 → one nonnegative scalar `A`, exact zero-sum `c=A(S+−S−)`, and the pooled out-of-fold scalar `alpha_0=max(0,WM(r/c;|c|))`. `safety.py` computes, in closed form and as pure functions, the uniform recent MAE excess risk, the signed Shape Wasserstein relevance, the exact break-even radii `lambda_U`/`lambda_S` from sorted `r/c` breakpoints, and `lambda=min(alpha_0,lambda_U,lambda_S)`; `history.py` holds the seven-record honest prequential bank. `W=7` is a structural constant with no capacity argument, warm start accepts OOF/prequential provenance only and refuses rather than shortening the window, and honesty is enforced from timestamps (`candidate_created_at < ordinal`). There is no learned gate, selector, uncertainty head, retrieval or market rule.

**This is a source refactor, so it produces no result and belongs in no results ledger.** `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` is deliberately unchanged, following the repository's own precedent that it records scientific diagnostics and no such diagnostic occurred. The closed verdict `HCH_SIGNED_MASS_DEVELOPMENT_NOT_SUPPORTED_STOP_FOR_ADJUDICATION`, all frozen evidence digests and all paper claims are unchanged; the refactor executed **zero** `DEV_EVAL` runs and read `PROTECTED_FINAL` **zero** times. ## Residual Alignment Safety consolidated — minimal generator + dual empirical safe-ray intersection; core refactor planned, no successor experiment authorized (2026-09-12)

Following the signed-mass closure and read-only QINGHAI diagnosis, the consolidated design authority is `docs/current/HCH_RESIDUAL_ALIGNMENT_SAFETY_DESIGN_20260912.md`, with math at `paper/02_math/RESIDUAL_ALIGNMENT_SAFE_RAY_DERIVATION_20260912.md`. The completed Signed-Mass experiment remains NOT SUPPORTED and is not rescued. The empirically surviving generator is now written directly in its minimal forward form: one shared tiny MLP, separate Shape/Amplitude GRU32 histories, two horizon-simplex Shapes and **one** nonnegative daily Amplitude scalar, giving `c=A(S+−S−)` and hence a zero-sum within-day redistribution correction. The prior deleted components (Shape semantic context, TCN, rare-mass sampling, untied ±Amplitude) remain deleted.

Deployment robustness is a non-trainable adjustment, not necessarily a novelty claim. `alpha_0` remains the exact chronological-OOF nonnegative MAE scalar. The safety history is fixed at **W=7 complete days**, justified as the shortest contiguous window covering one full weekly electricity-market cycle while remaining maximally local to deployment drift and matching the generator's existing memory; this is a structural default, not an optimality theorem, and the 7×H horizon coordinates are not treated as iid observations. Safety uses only honest persisted prequential `(S+,S-,c,r)` pairs. It computes both a uniform recent MAE break-even radius `lambda_U` and a Shape-weighted break-even radius `lambda_S`, then deploys `lambda=min(alpha_0,lambda_U,lambda_S)`. Thus Shape can only make deployment more conservative and cannot override broad recent harm. The selected scalar is empirically non-worse on both revealed recent risk surfaces, but **no future non-degradation/certificate claim is supported**. No learned Gate, uncertainty head, retrieval, deployment backprop, level-bias rescue or market-specific rule is introduced.

The source-only refactor authority is `docs/current/HCH_ALIGNMENT_SAFE_CORE_REFACTOR_PLAN_20260912.md`; the ready-to-execute implementation prompt is `docs/current/HCH_ALIGNMENT_SAFE_CORE_IMPLEMENTATION_PROMPT_20260912.md`. It requires byte-preserving archival of the current closed signed-mass core before replacing the active `src/core` with the minimal generator plus analytic safety primitives. Source implementation/tests are authorized by the user's design request, but **no new scientific DEV_EVAL run is authorized and `PROTECTED_FINAL` remains sealed.**

## Post signed-mass human adjudication — bottleneck shifts to correction-alignment stability; no successor experiment authorized (2026-09-12)

The signed-mass terminal verdict below remains unchanged. A read-only human adjudication of the frozen POST_TRAIN/DEV artifacts and the completed domestic baseline matrix is recorded at `docs/history/post_signed_mass_adjudication_20260912/README.md`. The new research bottleneck is **not another Shape/Amplitude architecture rescue**. In four markets the smallest signed-mass model extracts material reusable residual structure, while all four QINGHAI_DA Hosts fail despite identical recipe; exploratory raw-evidence diagnostics show QINGHAI applies non-trivial corrections whose deployment correlation with the realised Host residual is weak/negative, and its POST_TRAIN→DEV positive/negative residual-Shape centroid shift is the largest market-level pair in the panel. Across 20 cells, the exploratory association between Shape-centroid shift and MAE gain is negative, but NINGXIA is a decisive counterexample to any simple shift-threshold story. These are post-hoc diagnostics, not preregistered claims.

The narrower discussion question is therefore: **when does a frozen candidate correction remain aligned with deployment residual geometry, and can that alignment be assessed from causally revealed history without recreating a generic learned Gate?** This framing is supported by the domestic accuracy–safety spectrum: MDR/PIR show large negative transfer, δ-Adapter remains tightly Host-like, COSA online is safe but nearly inert, while signed-mass gains much more in 16/20 cells but fails hard under the QINGHAI regime. Generic fallback/gating, uncertainty-conditioned correction, online certificate calibration and retrieved-residual routing are already occupied by recent work, so any future route needs a smaller mathematical object such as correction–residual alignment / MAE-optimal causal shrinkage and an explicit collision audit before execution.

Current leading theory/design note: `docs/current/HCH_RESIDUAL_ALIGNMENT_SAFETY_DESIGN_20260912.md`; paper derivation: `paper/02_math/RESIDUAL_ALIGNMENT_SAFE_RAY_DERIVATION_20260912.md`. The safety layer is now explicitly a **supporting stabilizer, not required to be a novelty claim**. It keeps the minimal candidate generator fixed and reuses its predicted signed Shapes only as parameter-free relevance weights over **honest persisted prequential `(Shape,c,r)` pairs**. The proposed controller solves one exact Shape-weighted MAE ray problem: `A_align = 2Q+/Q - 1` is the loss-derived gate-like directional-support scalar; the deployed `lambda` is the largest value in `[0,alpha_0]` whose Shape-weighted recent excess MAE is non-positive. No new network, KNN residual retrieval, clipping firewall, learned selector or backpropagation is added. Replaying the current candidate on the same residual history that generated it is explicitly rejected as endogenous replay optimism. CRC full text has now been audited: its direction gate + quantile clipping + point-wise validation selection + shrink-to-base firewall strongly overlaps at the safety-objective level, so future paper wording must not claim safety/non-degradation novelty. **No successor experiment is authorized by this adjudication; `PROTECTED_FINAL` remains sealed.**

## Signed-mass 5-market × 4-Host development experiment closed — NOT SUPPORTED, all four components deleted (2026-09-12)

The first real signed-mass method experiment has run and closed on the complete domestic panel. Terminal verdict, uniquely determined by the preregistered rule: **`HCH_SIGNED_MASS_DEVELOPMENT_NOT_SUPPORTED_STOP_FOR_ADJUDICATION`**. This is a **development-experiment** result only; it is not a paper result and it does not license a paper claim. Evidence root: `experiments/evidence/hch_signed_mass_method_20260912/`; stage: `experiments/current/hch_signed_mass_method_entry/`; controlling prompt with the user's formal authorization: `AI_SCIENTIFIC_EXECUTION_PROMPT.md` (token `SIGNED_MASS_METHOD_EXECUTION_AUTHORIZED`). `PROTECTED_FINAL` was **not** authorized, is still sealed, and its read count is **0** in every artifact of the run.

Scope was the full **20-cell** panel — `GANSU_DA / SHANDONG_DA / SHAANXI_DA / NINGXIA_DA / QINGHAI_DA` × `PatchTST / TimeMixer / iTransformer / LSTM` — not a PatchTST/TimeMixer subset. 300 registered screen fits (5 configs × 20 cells × 3 seeds, 0 failed) plus 60 frozen-method fits (0 failed). Seeds were aggregated by **cell median** throughout; no best seed was ever selected.

The mandatory pre-run patch closed before any `DEV_EVAL` number existed: direct per-sign branch outputs now drive the mechanism metrics; the Amplitude decoder uses a training-only robust scale `s_A`; `q90(A⁺)/q90(A⁻)` high-mass thresholds are frozen from `POST_TRAIN` with a fit-row hash (20/20 cells, 0 degenerate); raw prediction evidence is immutable with `RAW_PREDICTION_INDEX.csv`; D0 ran on all 20 cells on `POST_TRAIN` only (`DEV_EVAL` never materialised, max reconstruction error `1.137e-13`); and an independent verifier recomputes everything from the artifacts. `runner.py verify` returned `SIGNED_MASS_CHINA5_METHOD_HARNESS_READY` at **367 checks / 0 failed** before the grid opened.

**All four preregistered components were deleted on their own five-condition rules, and no rescue variant was designed for any of them.** `shape_semantic_context` (1/20 cells) and `use_tcn` (2/20) fail all five conditions. `rare_mass_sampling` (9/20) fails conditions 1, 4 and 5. `untied_amplitude_heads` is the only component with real signal — `mass_negative_l1` median delta `−4.539` with 13/20 cells improved, median Overall-MAE delta `−1.1e-5`, conditions 1 and 3 TRUE — but it fails condition 2 (median `upper_tail_mae` delta `+1.564`, worse), condition 4 (one cell gains `+8.85` normal-region harm points from it alone) and condition 5 (only 1 seed carries a majority; ≥ 2 required). Every one of the four fails condition 4, and in every case the single driving cell is `GANSU_DA::LSTM` — the most asymmetric cell in D0 (A⁺ median `9.44` against A⁻ `3270`). The smallest surviving method is therefore the **empty switch vector** (all four OFF), label `FROZEN_SMALLEST`, route `FITTED_DERIVED_VECTOR`, `registered_config: null`: one shared feature stem, a window RNN, a softmax Shape head and a tied ± Amplitude head, 24,051–24,931 parameters, per-cell `POST_TRAIN` scalar `α ∈ [0.886, 1.809]`.

**Result:** **16/20** cells beat their own Host (median Overall-MAE gain **+8.434%**, median Tail-MAE gain **+7.624%**) and **16/20** beat the best setting-matched strict comparator (`delta-Adapter`, `OFFLINE_STATIC_POSTHOC`, in all 20 cells; in the 10 PatchTST/TimeMixer cells it also beat `PIR`, the second setting-matched candidate). The failure is concentrated entirely in **one market**: `QINGHAI_DA` loses to its own Host on all four Hosts (−7.392 / −8.235 / −9.826 / −26.689 %) and to `delta-Adapter` on all four, while every other market improves on every Host (+0.697 % to +15.930 %). The development gate gives **gate 1 FAIL** (16/20 host-nonworse against a required 19/20) and **gate 5 FAIL** (max cell-median normal-region relative harm **+7.585%** at `QINGHAI_DA::LSTM`, cap 1%); gates 2, 3, 4 and 6 pass. Negative-price MAE is numeric only in the four `SHANDONG_DA` cells; the other 16 have an empty negative-price subset and report `NOT_APPLICABLE_N0`, which is an empty subset, not a zero, and is not averageable across markets.

**Independent verification:** `verify-results` returned **PASS, 752 checks / 0 failed** over 360 artifacts (300 screen + 60 frozen), recomputing every metric from the raw arrays rather than echoing a stored verdict — 720 identity/hash checks, `n_algebra_violations: 0`, `n_metric_mismatches: 0`, **worst absolute recomputation difference `0.0` on every field**, derived verdict equal to the recorded verdict, and `n_excluded_from_rank: 20` (exactly the twenty `OFFLINE_STATIC_CONTROL` rows, the only rows in the frozen table the setting rule excludes). The strict rank is not thin: in the 10 PatchTST/TimeMixer cells `delta-Adapter` beat a second setting-matched candidate (`PIR`, `OFFLINE_STATIC_POSTHOC`, numeric on its two frozen backbones); in the 10 iTransformer/LSTM cells that PIR coordinate is `Q-INCOMPATIBLE` and has no row in the frozen table — the recorded thinness, not a new blocker.

**Disclosure — the audit instrument itself was corrected three times after its first pass**, and those corrections are recorded in the generated `FINAL_AUDIT.md` and `06_audits/`, not only in narration: (i) the Shape simplex tolerance had been a hardcoded float64 `1e-9` applied to rows that are a **float32** softmax, which failed all 300 Shape artifacts — measured across all 360 artifacts the residual ranges `5.96e-8 … 3.76e-7` with **0 above 1e-6** (float32 eps `1.19e-7`), so the tolerance is now `H · eps32` and a test still requires a denormalised Shape to fail at `O(1)`; (ii) the frozen method's own 60 fits were not previously audited; (iii) the frozen label was reconstructed from the switch vector as the placeholder `FROZEN` instead of being read from `FROZEN_METHOD_CONFIG.json`, which silently emptied the comparison join. None of the three alters a metric, a component decision or a gate. Separately, one implementation defect (`runner._dataset_for` returning a `(dataset, audit)` tuple to two callers that consume a bare dataset) was found and repaired before it produced any number.

**What this result does and does not establish.** It establishes that on this 20-cell panel, with the frozen Hosts and the frozen comparator table and no Host/baseline retraining, the signed-mass post-processor as implemented improves most cells but is not admissible as a paper candidate: two of the six gates fail, and both failures are traceable to a small number of cells rather than to a diffuse weakness. It does **not** establish that the signed-mass factorization is wrong, that any particular deleted component is useless in general, or that QINGHAI_DA is intrinsically unlearnable — no rescue variant, no per-market exemption and no metric substitution was run or is proposed, by rule. **No successor experiment is authorized by this result.** `PROTECTED_FINAL` remains sealed, and no Host or baseline may be retrained to chase it.

## Post-freeze NEM/NORD data sanity audit — NORD ready; NEM hourly-bin semantics require amendment before final Shape use (2026-09-12)

Independent post-freeze review accepts the international matrix execution itself: `NEM_NORD_BASELINE_MATRIX_FROZEN_WITH_INHERITED_BLOCKERS` remains valid (56/56 coordinates, 36 numeric, 20 inherited blockers, 8/8 Host gates, 13/13 pre-execution verifier, 39/39 final verifier, `PROTECTED_FINAL` read count 0). No frozen metric is withdrawn or rewritten.

`NORD_DK1` passes a strong external source-level sanity check. The local canonical 2023 series has average `86.8364 EUR/MWh`, minimum `-440.10`, maximum `524.27`, std `48.8149`, and `281` negative-price hours, essentially exactly matching the Danish Utility Regulator's 2023 report (`86.8 EUR/MWh`, `281` negative hours, minimum `-440.1`). On the frozen DEV window, a read-only previous-day persistence diagnostic gives MAE `27.8177`, versus frozen Hosts `19.8444/20.8733/20.5256/23.0470`; current status `PAPER_BENCHMARK_READY_CURRENT_STRICT`.

`NEM_SA1` raw AEMO source also passes market-level sanity. Local raw Q3-2024 five-minute diagnostics (mean `157.8446 AUD/MWh`, negative interval rate `28.2458%`, non-positive rate `29.3893%`, max `17500`) align with AEMO's Q3-2024 South Australia reporting. Its negative-heavy DEV window is present in the raw source rather than created by the models. Read-only previous-day persistence MAE is `80.4059`; TimeMixer `66.8525` and LSTM `71.1048` beat it while PatchTST/iTransformer are near it, so the Host scale is plausible.

However a real preprocessing/contract mismatch was found in the canonical NEM hourly product. AEMO `SETTLEMENTDATE` is interval-ending, while `build_clean.py` uses default `resample("h").mean()`: e.g. canonical row `01:00` averages the 12 raw ending stamps `01:00..01:55`, whereas a strict wall-clock hour should aggregate `01:05..02:00`. The frozen dataset contract incorrectly states `H:05..H:55` (11 samples), which matches neither the file nor a strict interval-ending hourly bin. This is not future leakage and does not break internal baseline comparability, but it can move spike/location information across hourly boundaries. A read-only corrected-bin sensitivity gives DEV mean abs target difference `8.928`, median `0.8525`, p95 `9.611`, max `1458.492`; rescoring frozen Host predictions changes aggregate MAE by only `+0.04..+0.17`, so headline MAE is robust while Shape/WHERE semantics are not guaranteed.

Therefore keep the frozen NEM matrix as valid comparison-infrastructure evidence under its actual canonical product, but do **not** use it as final paper evidence for signed-mass Shape / extreme-location claims until the hourly aggregation semantics are corrected in a new versioned canonical product and NEM-only Hosts/eligible baselines are rerun under the same protocol. Do not rerun NORD or reopen UEC/OMPB/PIR-new-Host blockers. Controlling audit note: `docs/current/NEM_NORD_POSTFREEZE_DATA_SANITY_AUDIT_20260912.md`.

Host qualification: PatchTST and TimeMixer were **imported only, never retrained** (all five markets). iTransformer is source-native against pinned official commit `c2426e68ca13f74aaec08045c5c724d8ad328124`, passing the ETTh1 96→96 anchor with both MSE and MAE inside 5% → `ITRANSFORMER_OFFICIAL_SOURCE_NUMERICALLY_ANCHORED`. LSTM is `CONVENTIONAL_INTERNAL_HOST / COMMON_CONTRACT_IMPLEMENTATION` and is nowhere called paper-faithful. MDR keeps `PROJECT_DEFINED_MATCHED_DIRECT_RESIDUAL_CONTROL`. δ-Adapter keeps `ACCEPTED_WITH_DISCLOSED_PARTIAL_FIDELITY`. PIR keeps `PAPER_FAITHFUL_EXACT_ACCEPTED` on the two frozen backbones but is Q-INCOMPATIBLE on the two new Hosts. COSA remains `ONLINE_TTA`.

Five findings worth carrying forward: (1) Host strength is market-dependent and does not order consistently — LSTM is the worst Host in four markets but the **best** in 宁夏 (75.73 vs PatchTST 82.47), and iTransformer is never best anywhere; (2) **MDR is worse than its own Host in 19 of 20 cells** (median −27.2%, worst −76.0%), i.e. the project-defined matched control is dominated by doing nothing; (3) δ-Adapter is within ±1.6% of its own Host in all 20 cells (better in 13) — consistent with a disclosed partial-fidelity light corrector; (4) PIR is worse in all 10 cells it can run (−5.9% to −50.6%) and never the best strict method; (5) COSA improves 19 of 20 cells by +0.01%…+1.71% (median +0.22%), with the largest gains on the weakest Host in each market — real but small, and online-only.

Seventeen repairs are logged (R-001…R-017), all class `Q-IMPL` (R-014 is a recorded blocker, not a repair). No seed, learning rate, epoch, hidden size, split, threshold, anchor, method variant or correction strength was changed after an outcome and no fidelity tolerance was relaxed; R-015…R-017 are evidence-writer and verifier-rule defects found by an independently written checker. Residual sign convention is disclosed (`pred − y_true` in the daily CSVs, matching the frozen parent; `y_true − pred` in the `.npz` arrays) and no reported metric depends on either.

## Signed-mass core source audit complete; registered method experiment is the next scientific stage (2026-09-12)

The user-authorized `src/core` reorganization has now been audited against the current signed-mass Shape/Amplitude design and is **source-ready for the registered development experiment**, but is still not scientifically promoted. Controlling method design: `docs/current/HCH_SIGNED_MASS_CONTEXT_ROUTED_NETWORK_DESIGN_20260912.md`. Next-stage experiment contract: `docs/current/HCH_SIGNED_MASS_METHOD_EXPERIMENT_ENTRY_PROTOCOL_20260912.md`. Source audit: `src/core/CORE_READINESS_AUDIT_20260912.md`.

The audit corrected several material information-contract defects before any method run: absent load was previously replaced by Host price; the first scaffold only exposed hard-coded load/wind/solar roles and would discard richer legal Chinese-market forecasts; historical Amplitude summaries were derived from normalized Shape channels and therefore did not preserve magnitude; only the first calendar coordinate was retained; the pooled MAE scalar had lost the registered nonnegative constraint; and the optional rare-positive oversampling pool mixed incorrect strata. These are now fixed. The core accepts one generic complete legal target-day `forecast_features (B,H,F)` tensor plus explicit availability masks, keeps all calendar coordinates, gives Shape scale-free residual history and Amplitude magnitude-preserving residual history, and uses `alpha=max(0, weighted_median(r/c; |c|))` per fitted market×Host repair model on legal chronological OOF rows.

The architecture is further simplified: all absolute legal target-day facts interact once in **one shared tiny MLP**; Shape alone receives additional scale-free within-day profile/ramp coordinates; Amplitude learns severity from the shared absolute embedding and magnitude-preserving residual history rather than from a hand-crafted severity inventory. Default executor remains small TCN→GRU32 in each branch, exact `c=A+S+−A−S−` fusion, no hidden-state Bridge and no learned proposal selector. KNN remains disabled and outside the first method.

Exactly four removable hypotheses are registered for the first experiment: Shape semantic context, TCN, untied positive/negative Amplitude heads, and rare-mass batch organization. Source switches exist solely for one-factor leave-one-out tests. **If a component fails its registered ablation it is deleted, not rescued.** Component screening is pre-registered on the available domestic markets × PatchTST/TimeMixer; the smallest surviving method is then frozen and appended to the already-running/frozen domestic Host×baseline substrate without baseline retraining. `PROTECTED_FINAL` remains sealed until a later human freeze.

Implementation readiness checks pass: current signed-mass source/purity/layout tests plus historical V2.5 runtime/readout/architecture compatibility tests pass together (53 tests at the audit checkpoint; final rerun follows source/doc freeze), and `src/core` compiles cleanly. No scientific method training, DEV_EVAL method result or protected/final evaluation was run by this audit, so `EXPERIMENT_LEDGER.md` is not advanced as a method result.

## Leading discussion design: signed-mass context-routed Shape/Amplitude repair (2026-09-12)

The research reset has converged to a narrower discussion-stage method object, documented at `docs/current/HCH_SIGNED_MASS_CONTEXT_ROUTED_NETWORK_DESIGN_20260912.md`. This is **not yet experimentally promoted**. The problem is no longer generic post-hoc forecasting or universal repair: for a frozen Host residual `r`, decompose positive/negative daily correction mass as `r = A+ S+ - A- S-`, where `S+ / S-` are horizon distributions answering **WHERE** correction should occur and `A+ / A-` answer **HOW MUCH** positive/negative correction mass the day requires. The factorization is identifiable via simplex-normalized Shapes and gives a direct MAE-aligned connection between branch errors and final residual error.

The current minimal executor is one shared tiny MLP stem over legal forecast-origin information, followed by task-directed semantic context and two small branch-local `TCN -> GRU32` paths. Shape receives scale-free profile/ramp/horizon context and predicts `S+ / S-` with softmax; Amplitude receives scale-sensitive level/stress/residual-severity context and predicts independent nonnegative `A+ / A-` heads. The branches share factual information, the common stem and final repair loss, but **do not use a hidden-state Bridge**. They combine only through deterministic `A+S+ - A-S-` fusion. The current pooled exact OOF MAE scalar `alpha` remains the preferred global calibration; it is not an instance-level Gate.

A learned Benefit/Trust/Repairability Gate remains excluded because the historical proposal-verification route failed strict OOF ordering and the same territory is crowded by PIR/CRC/other safe-correction work. Similar-window/KNN context is retained only as an evidence-gated Shape extension, not the default correction mechanism, because residual retrieval is already occupied by RATL-style methods. Rare high-mass Amplitude days are first handled as a training-data organization problem (stratified batch ordering, no duplication by default), not by a new expert/router. `TCN`, `GRU/BiLSTM`, similar-day retrieval and sampling are executors/training tools, not novelty claims.

The design still obeys **大道至简**: core scientific objects are the signed mass factorization and one global MAE calibration scalar. Before promotion, development-only evidence must show that Shape context improves location prediction, Amplitude context improves `A+ / A-`, independent upper/lower heads matter, TCN adds enough beyond the validated Daily-Patch GRU32 to justify itself, and rare-mass batch organization improves extremes without unacceptable Normal harm. `PROTECTED_FINAL` remains sealed during method selection.

A precomputed comparison substrate now exists for four Chinese provincial day-ahead markets — 山东 `SHANDONG_DA`, 陕西 `SHAANXI_DA`, 宁夏 `NINGXIA_DA`, 青海 `QINGHAI_DA` — so a future new method needs only to append an `Our Method` row without retraining a Host or a baseline. Semantics are strictly day-ahead price → day-ahead price next-24h; no historical DA→RT target or cache was reused anywhere.

Eight frozen Hosts (PatchTST, TimeMixer per market) all pass the three pre-registered sanity/fidelity gates, and the accepted GANSU_DA recipe was reused verbatim — architecture, loss and hyperparameters unchanged, only the dataset adapter and legal feature mapping differ per market, with no per-province search. Thirty-two strict offline cells (4 markets × 2 Hosts × {Host, MatchedDirectResidual, δ-Adapter Ada-Y, PIR}) and eight COSA cells are frozen with full raw predictions, targets, residuals and per-day 24h losses. Roles are chronological `HOST_TRAIN / HOST_VAL / POST_TRAIN / DEV_EVAL / PROTECTED_FINAL`; **no random split, no imputation, and `PROTECTED_FINAL` was never read** (structurally enforced by skiprows projection and verified per Host cache: only the four open roles appear). All scalers, quantiles and tail thresholds were fit on `HOST_TRAIN` only.

The substantive reading is a **Host-strength / repairability signal, and it is consistent across provinces**: only δ-Adapter ever matches or beats a frozen Host (best `+1.61%` on 陕西/PatchTST), MatchedDirectResidual is harmful everywhere (`-13%` to `-38%`), and PIR is harmful almost everywhere (`-5.9%` to `-50.6%`, the worst cells being the smallest `POST_TRAIN` markets 宁夏 and 青海). Harm concentrates in the normal-price region rather than the tail, and it grows as the baseline's available training block shrinks — the same shape as the `LAGO_PJM/PatchTST` failure that closed the transfer stage, now reproduced in four independent markets.

Coverage limits are disclosed, not repaired. 山西 `SHANXI` is **`ABSENT_FROM_REGISTRY`**: no Shanxi dataset exists in `data/CHINA/` or `data/MARKET_INDEX.csv`; it appears only as an archived `C_diagnostic_only` candidate with normalised 0-1 features and an undisclosed licence. It was not invented and not substituted, so the panel runs on the four markets that exist. `UEC_STD` is `HOST_FIDELITY_BLOCKED` (instantiating it needs a Host that failed admission, i.e. an unverified proxy) and `OMPB` is `STANDALONE_DATA_BLOCKED` (no reproducible standalone data path); neither was replaced by a proxy. Existing strict `GANSU_DA` evidence was imported by integrity audit only (`GANSU_DA_IMPORT_OK_NO_RETRAINING`, metric cross-check abs diff `0.0`) and was never retrained to tidy a table.

The strict offline main table stays limited to Host, MatchedDirectResidual, δ-Adapter and PIR; COSA is confined to the supplementary table carrying its `ONLINE_TTA_scored_on_adapted_days` setting and `TRANSFERRED_IMPLEMENTATION / PAPER_FIDELITY_UNRESOLVED` label, and UEC-STD/OMPB appear only as blocked rows. No fidelity adjudication was rewritten by any Chinese-province run. ## Host-Relative State Interaction rejected; S1 + pooled fixed alpha is the frozen structural candidate (2026-09-11)

The valid `HCH_HOST_RELATIVE_STATE_INTERACTION_NOT_SUPPORTED_FREEZE_S1` closure rejects the Host-relative state interaction as a universal Shape input. R0 validity and R4 efficiency pass; R1, R2 and R3 fail. This was the last authorized structural closure; no successor structure is open.

Method as executed: frozen S1 Shape reused unchanged; a causal Host-bias direction `b_hat` built from the seven-day same-hour normalized Host-residual history already carried by the frozen repair input (same-hour mean over the existing seven days, then L2-normalized over the 24 horizon hours; no decay, MAD, clipping or alternate window); the fixed five-role standardized state table `R in R^(24x5)` multiplied elementwise into `Z[h,k]=b_hat[h]*R[h,k]`; and **exactly one global `beta in R^5` shared by all six cells**, trained on the cross-market stacked chronological OOF pool with a cell-macro-balanced Shape loss. `u_HRSI = normalize(u_S1 + Z beta)` with `beta=0` replaying S1 at `1.192e-07`; frozen S1 metric replay exact at `1.421e-14`. `alpha_HRSI` refit per cell by the original exact weighted-median rule. Six cells: `GANSU_DA / LAGO_DE / LAGO_PJM × PatchTST / TimeMixer`, seeds 7/17/37. The whole six-cell panel trains **5 parameters**, with 0 per-cell beta vectors and 6 cells per beta.

The shared-beta constraint destroys the only mechanism that worked. GANSU_DA Shape cosine changes by only `+0.0002` (PatchTST) and `-0.0105` (TimeMixer) versus HSA's `+0.0666` / `+0.1170`, and both GANSU HIGH-state tertile cosines fall (`-0.0109` / `-0.0241`). Relative Overall-MAE gain vs S1 is nonnegative in only 1/6 cell medians and strictly better in only 1/6; the worst cell is `-0.6151` pp (LAGO_DE/PatchTST) and the worst seed `-1.3676` pp; 0/4 international cells are strictly better and only 1/6 cells are non-worse in `>=2/3` seeds. Median gain vs Host is `5.61%` with only 3/6 cells at `>=5%`, and maximum Normal-MAE harm is `1.0227%`, above the 1% gate. The learned beta is stable in sign and magnitude across seeds (seed7 `[0.109, 0.172, 0.000, -0.265, -0.134]`, seed17 `[0.048, 0.154, 0.000, -0.313, -0.050]`, seed37 `[0.054, 0.144, 0.000, -0.328, -0.047]`), but it is set by the four international cells, which contribute most of the pooled rows; `beta_SUPPLY_MARGIN_FC` is exactly `0.0` for every seed because that role is structurally absent in all three markets, so `Z[:,:,2]` is identically zero panel-wide.

Interpretation: HSA's GANSU gain came from per-market coefficient freedom. The scientific constraint that would make the interaction *universal* — one five-parameter beta for the entire panel — is exactly the constraint that removes it, and the pooled least-squares direction then degrades four cells to buy a `+0.15` pp GANSU/PatchTST change. Making the input Host-relative does not resolve the HSA transferability conflict; the conflict is in the coefficient, not the representation. Do not rescue this route by per-market/per-Host beta, per-hour beta, role selection, conditional Amplitude, a new window or transform, or post hoc threshold relaxation. **Freeze `S1 Daily-Patch GRU32 Shape + pooled fixed alpha` as the final structural candidate.** Evidence: `experiments/evidence/hch_host_relative_state_interaction_20260911/`; independent recomputation by `verify_gates.py` (does not import the runner) reproduces R0–R4 and the token from the written evidence alone at 50/50 integrity checks. This stage does not authorize SHAANXI/NINGXIA/QINGHAI, Shandong, or any full-panel/protected/final experiment.

The alpha row-set confound was measured rather than assumed: HRSI's scalar alpha lives on OOF folds B2–B4 (a beta cannot exist before B2) while frozen S1's pooled alpha uses B1–B4, so the comparison carries a discrete weighted-median shift of up to `26.39%`. Rescoring frozen S1 on HRSI's exact row set makes HRSI look **worse**, not better: non-worse in only 3/6 cell medians, worst cell `-0.7848` pp, worst seed `-1.3998` pp. The failure is therefore not an artifact of the alpha row set. That diagnostic is disclosed in `alpha_rowset_sensitivity.csv` and never feeds a gate.

Baseline comparison infrastructure is considered complete for the current method stage: all selected comparison methods have completed their strict paper-protocol/official-code/split/training/metric consistency audit, while exact admission status and any fidelity limitations remain those recorded in the final baseline adjudication. Later HCH-vs-baseline experiments must use identical dataset/task/Host/evaluation contracts and must not reopen baseline search/tuning.

## Horizon-Aligned Semantic State Residual rejected; S1 + pooled fixed alpha retained (2026-09-11)

The valid `HCH_HORIZON_ALIGNED_STATE_RESIDUAL_NOT_SUPPORTED_KEEP_S1` closure rejects the five-role horizon-aligned semantic state residual as a universal Shape input. H0 validity and H4 efficiency pass; H1, H2 and H3 fail.

Method as executed: frozen S1 OOF Shape directions reused unchanged; a deterministic `R_d in R^(24x5)` role table mean-pooled from audited `primary_channel=True` forecast-time state columns into `DEMAND_FC / RENEWABLE_FC / SUPPLY_MARGIN_FC / INTERCHANGE_FC / MUST_RUN_FC` (structurally absent role = 0, `SUPPLY_MARGIN_FC` absent in all three markets); the only new trainable object is `beta in R^5` with `u_HSA = normalize(u_S1 + R_d beta)`; `alpha_HSA` refit with the original exact weighted-median rule on HSA OOF directions. `beta=0` replays S1 at `1.192e-07`; S1 metric replay is exact at `1.421e-14`. Six cells: `GANSU_DA / LAGO_DE / LAGO_PJM × PatchTST / TimeMixer`, seeds 7/17/37.

The mechanism is real but local. Both GANSU_DA Hosts improve Shape cosine (`+0.0666` PatchTST, `+0.1170` TimeMixer) and both improve the frozen GANSU HIGH-state tertile (`+0.0347` / `+0.1189`) while cutting HIGH-state wrong-hemisphere by 12.5 / 18.2 pp. GANSU Overall-MAE beats S1 by `+1.30` / `+4.31` pp and reaches `3.55%` / `11.04%` gain vs Host. But the gain is not universal: cell-median Shape cosine rises in only 4/6 cells and wrong-hemisphere is non-worse in only 3/6; relative Overall-MAE gain vs S1 is nonnegative in only 4/6 cell medians (worst `-0.87` pp on LAGO_DE/PatchTST); median gain vs Host is `6.73%` with only 3/6 cells at `>=5%`; normal-MAE harm peaks at `1.02%`, just over the 1% gate; and only 3/6 cells hold HSA `>=` S1 in `>=2/3` seeds.

`beta` is fitted per market on that market's own chronological OOF rows only, with a single shared five-role vocabulary; no market ID, Host ID, role embedding, per-hour beta, hidden layer, bias, LR/step search, excursion/MAD feature, or market-specific rule is used. Structurally absent roles keep `beta=0` exactly, so LAGO_PJM fits only `DEMAND_FC` and LAGO_DE only `DEMAND_FC`/`RENEWABLE_FC`. Efficiency is not the bottleneck: 5 extra parameters, median beta training `1.73 s/cell`, median inference overhead `1.22 us/day`.

Interpretation: horizon-aligned semantic state supplies usable directional information for GANSU_DA but is not a mechanism that transfers without market-specific behaviour. Do not rescue this route by adding China-only roles, per-market role selection, per-hour or conditional `beta`, larger step budgets, or by relaxing the H1/H2/H3 thresholds post hoc. Keep `S1 Daily-Patch GRU32 Shape + pooled fixed alpha` as the executable method. Evidence: `experiments/evidence/hch_horizon_aligned_state_residual_20260911/`; independent recomputation by `verify_gates.py` (does not import the runner) reproduces H0–H4 and the token from the written evidence alone at 39/39 integrity checks. ## State-Excursion Shape rejected; Horizon-Aligned Semantic State Residual authorized (2026-09-11)

The valid `HCH_STATE_EXCURSION_SHAPE_NOT_SUPPORTED_KEEP_S1` closure rejects the signed same-hour seven-day excursion representation as a universal Shape input. S2 improves all four international cells relative to S1 but degrades both `GANSU_DA` cells; GANSU Shape cosine falls and wrong-hemisphere does not improve. Do not rescue this route by changing the 7-day window, MAD scaling constant `1.4826`, clipping range, or market-specific state-role selection. The `1.4826` factor is only the standard Gaussian-consistency MAD rescaling and remains diagnostic-only.

The stronger interpretation is a horizon-alignment / representation-efficiency bottleneck. S1 flattens the complete target-day state trajectory into one current-day token before a 32-D projection and 24-D readout; parameter count grows with raw channel count (~14.1k PJM, ~14.9k DE, ~18.7k GANSU) and S2 increases this imbalance further (~23.3k GANSU), while GANSU has only 62 DIAG_FIT days. The next smallest justified change is therefore not another state transform or deeper network.

Authorized closure: `docs/current/HCH_HORIZON_ALIGNED_STATE_RESIDUAL_SHAPE_CLOSURE_20260911.md`. Freeze S1 entirely and fit exactly five shared semantic-role coefficients on top of chronological OOF S1 unit Shape: build a fixed `24x5` role-state table from the existing S1 train-only standardized state levels (`DEMAND_FC / RENEWABLE_FC / SUPPLY_MARGIN_FC / INTERCHANGE_FC / MUST_RUN_FC`), set `q=R beta`, and `u_HSA=normalize(u_S1+q)`. `beta=0` initially replays S1; the same five parameters and training rule apply in every market. Refit only the existing pooled exact MAE scalar alpha on HSA OOF directions. No conditional Amplitude, learned Verification, excursion/MAD features, deeper Shape network, market-specific rules, extra markets, Shandong, or protected/final access.
